## Appendix B. Research-agent findings by cluster

Fourteen clusters were commissioned; the clusters below returned structured findings before this report was finalized. Each agent was instructed to read primary pages through the fetch service, label every claim fact / historical / estimate / unknown, and record the source URL and date. The author re-verified the consequential claims against the primary pages (Appendix A) before using them in the body; where an agent figure was not re-verified it is reported here as the agent stated it. Confidence is the agent's own.

### B.1 Puget Sound market

*Scope:* Puget Sound (King, Snohomish, Pierce; Bellevue/Kirkland/Redmond/Seattle Eastside) as baseline growth market for Integra Dental Design — dentist supply, named implant/digital practices, local labs and milling centers, DSOs, pricing/turnaround norms, study-club channels, switching barriers (desk research, accessed 2026-10-12)

**Agent summary.** Bottom line: the three-county Puget Sound market is large, dense in dentists, and already served by roughly 30 named independent labs in King County alone plus national mail-order labs, DSO in-house labs, and full-arch chains with on-site labs. There is no evidence it is underserved. Integra's realistic edge is proximity to a Bel-Red corridor cluster of prosthodontists, in-house 5-axis milling including titanium, and published flat pricing — not scarcity of competitors. FACTS (primary sources). Washington had 7,071 dentist licenses in 2024 (UW CHWS using DOH licensure). King County's ratio was 112 licensed dentists per 100,000 (2023; 110 in the 2022 ACH brief), versus under 70 everywhere else in the state; Elevate Health (Pierce) was 55 and North Sound ACH (Snohomish plus four counties) 62 per 100,000 in 2022. DOH's 2023 legislative report puts King at ~1,000 patients per provider and Pierce at ~1,600. OFM April 1, 2025 populations: King 2,411,700; Pierce 959,900; Snohomish 873,800. Society memberships: SKCDS "over 1,600" dentists (WSDA takes over SKCDS management Sept 24, 2026; contact skcds@wsda.org); Snohomish County Dental Society "over 380"; Pierce County Dental Society "over 385" (~55% of Pierce dentists). Nationally, 21.2% of active dentists are specialists (ADA HPI 2025), so ~79% are GPs. ESTIMATES. Applying the UW ratio to OFM population gives ~2,630 dentist license addresses in King County (2023) and ~2,700 at 2025 population; Pierce ~530–600 (license-based) or ~540–700 (society-implied); Snohomish ~540 (if it matches the North Sound ACH average) to ~690 (society-implied). Three-county total ~3,700–4,000 licenses, of which ~2,900–3,150 are GPs. Caveat from the source: license mailing address is not practice location and includes non-practicing dentists. LABS. Desk research identified ~29 named labs in King County (Nakanishi, Protea, PNW Pros, Prime, Denny, New Era, Novus, Smile Design, Skylab, Shama, Seattle Dental Arts, Swiss Dental, Thorn Ford, First Impressions/Dentcron, Cox, New Smile, Paragon, Choice, Emerald, VTA-Renton, United, Ray Dental Studio, Cosmetic Dental Arts, Athena, Oral Esthetics, Tom Cummings, ProWest, Issaquah Dental Lab, Integra), 4+ in Snohomish (Seabrook, Oasis, Trosvig, Edgewood) and at least two in Pierce (University Park, Tacoma Dental Laboratories), plus Ziemek (Olympia) with its own milling center. That is roughly one identified lab per ~90 King County dentists — a floor, since the DOH Dental Laboratory Registration database could not be queried and the Census CBP API requires a key. No national-baseline comparison could be sourced, so "underserved" is not supportable. Only Integra publishes a public fee schedule locally; every other local lab quotes privately. National labs reach the area by mail (Glidewell: $9 FedEx each way or free USPS 2–4 days each way; Pro-Craft of Murrieta CA sells WA dentists a "free iTero" program with 3–5 day turnaround; Dandy has a Kirkland testimonial). NDX lists no WA lab; Issaquah Dental Laboratory (founded 1973) shows as acquired/PE-backed and its NDX page now redirects, so its status is unknown. DSOs. PDS: 40 WA offices (Becker's, ~2023) running CEREC same-day crowns in-office. Willamette Dental Group: in-house lab (milled crowns, printed dentures) with WA offices in Bellevue, Everett, Kent, Mountlake Terrace, Puyallup, Seattle, Tacoma and 11 other cities. Light Dental Studios: 30 Puget Sound offices with an in-house denture lab. Gentle Dental 27 WA offices; Bright Now ~19. ClearChoice Metro-Seattle (Bellevue) and Nuvia Bellevue each run on-site labs and capture full-arch volume. Affordable Dentures has on-site denture labs in Tacoma and Lynnwood. PRICING/TURNAROUND BENCHMARKS (published, mostly out-of-area): monolithic zirconia $89–$122 (Crown World, ROE, TrueGuide), lab-to-lab zirconia $39–$59 (Mabel), screw-retained zirconia implant crowns $299–$349, full-arch zirconia finals $1,999–$2,420. Integra's $129 crown is at the upper edge of national mail-order pricing, its $22 mill-only is well below lab-to-lab norms, and its $1,700 arch is competitive. Turnaround parity is 5–7 business days; local labs differentiate on free courier pickup/delivery (Oasis, Choice, Seabrook, CBI). CHANNELS. SKCDS/SCDS/PCDS general meetings (PCDS sells sponsorships; Nakanishi sponsored April 2026), WSDA's PNDC (May 28–30, 2026, Seattle Convention Center, ~150 exhibitors), Seattle Study Club HQ in Kirkland with WA affiliates (Seattle Digital Dental Hub, ISGNW), Kois Center and four local Kois study groups (incl. Protea's in Redmond), Seattle Implant Study Club (Lynnwood, AAID-affiliated), 47 North Oral Surgery's restorative-implant mini-residency, Puget Sound Perio's South Sound study club, Washington AGD (SeaTac), WSDLA (board includes Protea's Leon Hermanides), and UW Graduate Prosthodontics (Interim Director Dr. John Sorensen, fully digital All-on-4 research lab; residents print models). SWITCHING BARRIERS. Decades-long lab loyalty is documented in local testimonials; in-office milling is spreading (PDS, Bellevue Premier Dental, Sunrise Redmond, Lynnwood Dental Excellence); national labs subsidize scanners; full-arch chains and DSOs are vertically integrated. A dentist would try Integra for proximity (Drs. Najafi and Libman and two labs sit within ~1 mile), titanium milling, transparent pricing and owner access — not because alternatives are missing. Integra's concentration risk (2 clients = 91%) and thin cash are credibility issues a skeptical buyer will probe.

**Findings.**

- *fact, high confidence.* Washington had 7,071 dentist licenses in 2024 (DENT, DEFC, DERE, DEUW), up from 6,886 in 2022. Value: 7,071 licenses (2024). [source](https://familymedicine.uw.edu/chws/resources/wohw/workforce-data/current-supply/) (undated page (data through 2024); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* King County (Healthier Here ACH) had 112 licensed dentists per 100,000 population in 2023; all other ACHs were below 70. Value: 112 per 100,000 (King, 2023); 110 per 100,000 (2022 chart). [source](https://familymedicine.uw.edu/chws/resources/wohw/workforce-data/current-supply/) (undated (2023 data); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Pierce County (Elevate Health ACH) had 55 and North Sound ACH (Snohomish, Skagit, Whatcom, Island, San Juan) 62 licensed dentists per 100,000 in 2022. Value: Pierce 55/100k; North Sound 62/100k (2022). [source](https://familymedicine.uw.edu/chws/wp-content/uploads/sites/5/2025/01/Data-Brief-Dentist-Distribution-and-Population-Dental-Need-2022.pdf) (November 2024; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* DOH reports ~74 dentists per 100,000 statewide, with King County at ~1,000 patients per provider and Pierce at ~1,600. Value: King ~1,000 pts/dentist; Pierce ~1,600 pts/dentist. [source](https://doh.wa.gov/sites/default/files/2023-09/140265-OralHealthEquityAssessment.pdf) (September 2023; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* OFM April 1, 2025 county populations: King 2,411,700; Pierce 959,900; Snohomish 873,800 (King 2023: 2,347,800). Value: King 2,411,700; Pierce 959,900; Snohomish 873,800. [source](https://ofm.wa.gov/sites/default/files/public/dataresearch/pop/april1/ofm_april1_press_release.pdf) (June 27, 2025; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *estimate, medium confidence.* Estimated licensed dentists: King ~2,630 (2023) to ~2,700 (2025); Pierce ~530–700; Snohomish ~540–690; three-county ~3,700–4,000, of which ~2,900–3,150 are general dentists. Value: ~2,630–2,700 King; ~3,700–4,000 three-county; ~2,900–3,150 GPs. [source](https://familymedicine.uw.edu/chws/resources/wohw/workforce-data/current-supply/) (derived 2026-10-12 from 2022–2025 sources)
- *fact, high confidence.* ADA HPI: 205,088 professionally active U.S. dentists in 2025, 60.0 per 100,000, 21.2% in an ADA-recognized specialty. Value: 60.0 dentists/100k US; 21.2% specialists. [source](https://www.ada.org/resources/research/health-policy-institute/dentist-workforce) (2025 update; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Seattle-King County Dental Society has 'over 1,600' member dentists; WSDA will provide its association management from Sept 24, 2026. Value: >1,600 members. [source](https://www.skcds.org/) (undated (references Sept 2026 events); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Snohomish County Dental Society has over 380 members; Pierce County Dental Society over 385 members (~55% of Pierce dentists). Value: SCDS >380; PCDS >385 (or >430 per LinkedIn). [source](https://pcdentists.org/p/Dental-Society-Pierce-County-Washington-About-Us-p32339.asp) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *estimate, medium confidence.* At least 29 named dental labs operate in King County, ~4 in Snohomish and ~2 in Pierce, i.e., roughly one identified lab per ~90 King County dentists (a floor). Value: ~29 King County labs identified; ~1 per 90 dentists (floor). [source](https://www.enigma.com/directory/wa/all-cities/dental-laboratory/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Nakanishi Dental Laboratory (Bellevue, 2959 Northup Way) is a full-service lab with 'many Certified Dental Technicians', ships nationwide, and sponsors county dental-society meetings. Value: revenue band $1M–$5M (directory estimate). [source](https://www.nakanishidentallab.com/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Protea Dental Studio (Redmond) is a Kois-affiliated lab run by Leon Hermanides CDT, hosts a Kois study club, and Hermanides is WSDLA Past President. [source](https://www.proteadental.com/kois-study-club/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* PNW Pros Dental Lab (Bellevue) is owned/led by two prosthodontists who do implant surgical planning, guides and full-arch work; DOH registration DLAB.FS.61146491. [source](https://pnwproslab.com/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Ray Dental Studio (Kent) mills custom titanium abutments in-house and offers lifetime warranty on BruxZir/Origin Live zirconia; Choice Dental Lab (Renton) runs driver pickup/delivery and full-arch zirconia; Emerald Dental Arts (Renton) mills Ti/CoCr bars. [source](https://www.raydentalstudio.net/services) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Lynnwood-area labs Oasis and Seabrook offer free local pickup and delivery; Oasis charges no rush fee and covers Seattle, Bellevue and Arlington. Value: free local pickup/delivery; no rush charge (Oasis). [source](https://www.oasisdentallabllc.com/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* VTA Dental Lab (multi-state) keeps a Renton office at 901 Rainier Ave N Ste B103 and advertises 7-day zirconia crowns; Pro-Craft (Murrieta, CA) targets WA dentists with a free iTero program and 3–5 business-day delivery. Value: Pro-Craft 3–5 business days; VTA 7-day zirconia. [source](https://www.pro-craft.com/dental-laboratory-bellevue-washington) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Glidewell has no Washington lab; it serves WA by mail — FedEx Overnight $9 each way or free prepaid USPS Priority Mail taking 2–4 business days each way; in-office pickup for existing customers. Value: $9/way FedEx; USPS 2–4 days each way. [source](https://glidewelldental.com/send-case/pickup-and-delivery) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *unknown, medium confidence.* National Dentex (NDX) lists no Washington or Oregon laboratory; Issaquah Dental Laboratory (640 NW Gilman Blvd, founded 1973) appears acquired/PE-backed and its former NDX page now redirects. [source](https://nationaldentex.com/labs) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Dandy manufactures in Lehi, UT and Carrollton, TX and has at least one Kirkland, WA practice testimonial (Smiles Dental Services); it supplies a scanner against a lab minimum. [source](https://www.meetdandy.com/labs/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *historical, medium confidence.* Pacific Dental Services supports 40 Washington offices and runs CEREC same-day crowns in-office across supported practices. Value: 40 WA offices. [source](https://www.beckersdental.com/dso-dpms/pacific-dental-services-network-state-by-state-breakdown/) (~2023 (article ~1,200 days old at access); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Willamette Dental Group operates an in-house dental lab (digitally scanned and milled ceramic crowns, 3D-printed dentures) and has Washington offices in 18 cities including Bellevue, Everett, Kent, Mountlake Terrace, Puyallup, Seattle and Tacoma. Value: 18 WA cities. [source](https://locations.willamettedental.com/wa/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Light Dental Studios runs 30 Puget Sound locations (incl. Bellevue, Kent, Lynnwood, Burien, Covington, Auburn) with an in-house denture lab serving all offices; Gentle Dental 27 WA offices; Bright Now ~19 WA offices; Affordable Dentures & Implants has on-site denture labs in Tacoma and Lynnwood. Value: LDS 30; Gentle 27; Bright Now ~19. [source](https://lightdentalstudios.com/dental-services/) (page dated 2024-09-04; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* ClearChoice Metro-Seattle (1200 112th Ave NE, Bellevue) and Nuvia Bellevue each operate on-site labs and capture full-arch zirconia volume internally. [source](https://www.clearchoice.com/locations/wa/bellevue/1200-112th-ave-ne/) (undated (reviews dated May 2026); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* No Puget Sound lab other than Integra publishes a public fee schedule; local pricing norms must be inferred from national published lists. [source](https://integra.dental/fee-schedule/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Published national benchmarks for monolithic zirconia crowns run $89–$122 (digital) and screw-retained zirconia implant crowns $299–$349; Integra's $129 crown sits at the upper edge of mail-order pricing. Value: $89–$122 FCZ; $299–$349 screw-retained zirconia. [source](https://crownworlddental.com/lab-services-2025/) (2025-09-10 (Crown World); 2026 (ROE); 2026-09-24 (TrueGuide); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Published lab-to-lab zirconia pricing is $39 (coping) to $59 (full contour) with 6-day digital turnaround; Integra's $22 mill-only is far below this benchmark. Value: $39–$59 lab-to-lab zirconia; $1,999 full-arch zirconia. [source](https://mabeldental.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Mabel-Dental-Lab-to-Lab-1-2026-Fee-Schedule.pdf) (January 2026; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Published full-arch zirconia final pricing ranges from $1,999 (Mabel) to $2,420 (ROE TLZ-IB) and $2,888 (ROE NobelProcera); ADL lists $3,750 for a zirconia bridge with gingival up to 5 sites. Value: $1,999–$3,750 per arch (published, out-of-area). [source](https://www.roedentallab.com/hubfs/47353199/PDFs/Fee-Schedule-ROE.pdf) (2026 (REV100726); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Patient-facing implant fees on the Eastside: Klahanie Family Dentistry (Issaquah) lists $5,700 for a posterior implant+abutment+crown, $6,500 anterior, $12,000 for 4–6 implants under an existing denture. Value: $5,700 posterior single implant+crown. [source](https://klahaniedental.com/dental-implants/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Turnaround parity in this market is 5–7 business days in-lab for zirconia; Integra's five-day promise is parity, not a differentiator. Value: 5–7 in-lab days typical. [source](https://vtadentallab.com/dental-lab-in-washington/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* How cases move: local labs use their own drivers (free pickup/delivery routes), national labs use prepaid USPS Priority or $9 FedEx Overnight, and digital cases go via 3Shape Communicate / Medit Link / iTero connections. [source](https://www.choice-lab.com/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Washington requires every dental lab doing business in the state to register with DOH (since Jan 31, 2021); new registrants after Jan 31, 2025 must employ a CDT working 30+ hours/week or operate under a licensed dentist; renewal fee $250. Value: $250 annual renewal. [source](https://doh.wa.gov/licenses-permits-and-certificates/facilities-z/dental-laboratories) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* In-office milling is established on the Eastside: Bellevue Premier Dental (CEREC, iTero, 3D printer, CBCT), Sunrise Dental of Redmond (CEREC, '98% same-day crown delivery'), Lynnwood Dental Excellence (in-house 5-axis lab-grade mill), PDS (CEREC in all offices). [source](https://www.lynnwooddentalcenter.com/lynnwood-dental-implants-technology-in-house-5-axis-dental-laboratory-grade-milling.html) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Documented lab loyalty is long: Seattle Dental Arts 'lab of choice for over 20 years' (Dr. Linda Fukuda), Emerald Dental Arts 15 years (Dr. Isenberg), Paragon Dental Art 10 years, Pacific Dental Arts 13 years/~5,000 units from one dentist. [source](https://seattledentalarts.com/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* The Bel-Red corridor within ~1 mile of Integra (35 148th Ave SE) holds a cluster of prosthodontists and labs: Dr. Siamak Najafi (14645 Bel-Red Rd Ste 100), Dr. Warren Libman (14595 Bel-Red Rd Ste 100), Prime Dental Lab (14595 Bel-Red Rd), Denny Dental Lab (14420 NE Bel-Red Rd), Bellevue Dental Arts (13333 Bel-Red Rd), Bellevue Premier Dental (1951 152nd Pl NE). [source](https://libmandds.com/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Seattle Met's 2026 peer-voted Top Dentists prosthodontics list names ~35 Puget Sound prosthodontists, including Bellevue (Russell & Michael Johnson, Warren Libman, Rosario Palacios), Mercer Island (Shiotsu), Lynnwood (Raigrodski), Federal Way (Chong; ClearChoice's Piñeyro), Puyallup (Larson), Tacoma (Phillips) and UW faculty (Ramos, Schwedhelm). Value: ~35 prosthodontists listed. [source](https://www.seattlemet.com/dentists/type/prosthodontics) (2026 list; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* WSDA's Pacific Northwest Dental Conference 2026 runs May 28–30 at the Summit, Seattle Convention Center, with ~150 exhibitors. Value: ~150 exhibitors; May 28–30, 2026. [source](https://withorbital.com/conferences/pacific-northwest-dental-conference-2026) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Seattle Study Club is headquartered in Kirkland (9805 NE 116th St #7316; 425-576-8000), runs 250+ affiliate clubs meeting 8–10 times/year, and sells an industry partner/sponsor program; Washington affiliates include Seattle Digital Dental Hub (Dr. Rooz Khosravi), Interdisciplinary Study Group Northwest (Dr. Helena; Kois-based; Tacoma/Sumner/Auburn area) and Spokane Study Club. [source](https://www.seattlestudyclub.com/contact) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* The Kois Center (1001 Fairview Ave N, Seattle) anchors four local Kois study groups — Western Washington Kois Study Group, MDB Kois Study Club, WACRE (full), Protea Dental Kois Study Club (Redmond) — and Kois Dentistry (Drs. John, Dean, Tara Kois) is a full-arch/implant practice. [source](https://www.koiscenter.com/members-alumni/kois-study-groups/) (page dated 2017-04-28 (listing may be stale); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Seattle Implant Study Club (AAID-affiliated) meets in Lynnwood (19720 68th Ave W Ste A; 425-776-9132); 2025 president Dr. Brian Baik; 47 North Oral Surgery runs a four-session, 28-CE 'Graduate Synergy Training Program' mini-residency for restorative dentists from Kirkland/Redmond/Monroe; Puget Sound Periodontics runs the South Sound Multi-Disciplinary Study Club. [source](https://www.seattleimplantstudyclub.com/) (undated (2025 president); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Washington AGD operates a Global Learning Center in SeaTac with MasterTrack and hands-on courses (Director of Education Dr. Timothy Hess; washingtonagd.org); no Spear or ITI study club in Seattle/Bellevue/Kirkland was found. [source](https://www.agd.org/education/awards-recognition/agd-mastership/mastertrack-programs/mastertrack-programs/washington) (Dec 15, 2022 entry; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* UW School of Dentistry is a digital-prosthodontics channel: Graduate Prosthodontics (Interim Director Dr. John Sorensen, ex-ClearChoice Portland, B4T research lab pursuing a fully digital All-on-4 workflow; lab technician Kleve Hansen; residents print models and use sintering ovens) and a CDE program with ceramics/full-arch webinars in Nov 2026. [source](https://dental.washington.edu/graduate-prosthodontics/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Eastside practices naming their scanners: Kirkland Premier Dentistry (iTero, CBCT; prosthodontist + oral surgeon in-house), Issaquah Premier Dental (Primescan, Primescan 2, iTero Lumina), Bellevue Dental Arts (iTero Lumina Pro), Redmond DDS (Primescan), Smiles By Design Kirkland (TRIOS), ARID Northwest Lynnwood (TRIOS), Blooming Dental Lynnwood/Des Moines (iTero, CBCT, guided surgery). [source](https://www.ziemekdentallab.com/products-services/what-we-do/) (undated; accessed 2026-10-12)

Value: 109/100k King (2016). [source](https://depts.washington.edu/fammed/chws/wp-content/uploads/sites/5/2017/11/Washington_State_Oral_Health_Workforce_FR_Nov_2017_Patterson.pdf) (November 2017; accessed 2026-10-12)

**Unknowns the agent could not resolve.**

- Exact count of DOH-registered dental laboratories in King, Snohomish and Pierce counties — the DOH Provider Credential/Facility Search (Facility Type: Dental Laboratory Registration) is interactive and could not be queried; no published total was found.
- Census County Business Patterns establishment/employment counts for NAICS 339116 (dental laboratories) and 621210 (offices of dentists) by county — the Census API returned 'Missing Key'; BLS QCEW CSV endpoints were blocked by content type. Without these, no lab-to-dentist ratio versus a national baseline can be stated.
- Snohomish County-specific dentist ratio (only the five-county North Sound ACH figure of 62/100k is published); exact licensed dentist counts by county (UW tableau 'County Trends' not fetchable).
- Pierce County Dental Society membership and penetration — its own site says >385 members (~55%), LinkedIn says >430 (~80%); neither is dated.
- Whether Issaquah Dental Laboratory (640 NW Gilman Blvd) still operates and under whose ownership (PitchBook: acquired/PE-backed; former NDX page redirects).
- Whether Glidewell has any milling or pickup presence in Kent, WA (a 2023 forum post claimed so; no corporate evidence).
- Actual fee schedules and turnaround commitments of local labs (Nakanishi, Protea, Shama, Seattle Dental Arts, Smile Design, Prime, PNW Pros, Choice, Oasis, Seabrook, Trosvig, Emerald, Ray, Ziemek) — none publish prices; United Dental Lab mails one in its starter kit.
- Which labs the named Eastside prosthodontists and full-arch practices currently use, and whether Washington State Prosthodontics (Drs. Johnson) routes work to the affiliated PNW Pros lab.
- Aspen Dental and Heartland Dental office counts in Washington; whether Gentle Dental (InterDent) or Bright Now use centralized labs.
- Cost of exhibiting at PNDC 2026, sponsoring SKCDS/SCDS/PCDS general membership meetings, or joining Seattle Study Club's partner program (no public rate cards found).
- Current status of Seattle-area Spear or ITI study clubs (none found) and of UW's Implant Systems Study Club (last evidence 2015).
- Any evidence of dentist dissatisfaction with existing local labs — none was found; claims of unmet need would be speculation.

### B.2 Puget Sound costs and taxes

*Scope:* Cost of growing in place in Puget Sound (Bellevue / Eastside / South King County) for Integra Dental Design: rent, WA taxes, payroll loads, wages, sales-rep comp, incremental equipment, local marketing, shipping/courier

**Agent summary.** Bottom line for a skeptical reader: growing in place is cheap on taxes and payroll loads, expensive on rent-per-foot and on the one thing Integra actually lacks (sales capacity). The numbers below are as of 2026-10-12. FACTS (primary sources). Rent: Integra's $2,300/mo for ~600 SF is $3.83/SF/mo all-in, well above market for flex. The Tukwila Seattle Exchange park (Lee & Associates, CBA listings modified 8/14 and 9/22/2026) is asking $2.10/SF/mo blended, modified gross ($2,961/mo for 1,410 SF; $4,450/mo for 2,119 SF) with a published promo of $1.00/SF/mo for the first year on eligible suites. Redmond Willows flex asks $1.80-$2.04/SF/mo NNN with 2026 NNN of $0.57 (95th Street Tech Center) or $2.40/SF gross on a short-term sublease (WillowsFlex, Sept 2026-Oct 2027). Kent Valley small bays go for $1.55 NNN; Kidder Mathews Q2 2026 puts Eastside direct asking at $1.88 NNN vs Southend $1.01-$1.05, with Eastside vacancy 6.0% and regional 9.5% (tenant-favorable; landlords trading concessions for occupancy). Bellevue office/retail small suites on 148th Ave SE run $34/SF/yr + ~$10.80 NNN (about $3.73/SF/mo). Taxes: WAC 458-20-151 classifies lab fabrication as manufacturing (0.484%) plus retailing (0.471%, sales to dentists) or wholesaling (0.484%, lab-to-lab with reseller permit), with the MATC so tax is paid once; rates rise to 0.50% on 1/1/2027 (ESHB 2081). Dental prostheses are sales-tax exempt (RCW 82.08.0283) and mills/furnaces/printers qualify for the M&E exemption (RCW 82.08.02565), worth 10.3% in Bellevue. The small-business B&O credit ($55/mo for non-service filers, RCW 82.04.4451) wipes out state B&O at Integra's current ~$140k/yr and phases out around $280k/yr. Bellevue adds a city B&O of 0.1596% above $215,000 taxable receipts, a square-footage tax of $0.3297475/sq ft/quarter on support/out-of-city-sales space, and a $119 license. Service revenue (training, design-only) is 1.5%. Payroll: L&I class 3602 (dental labs) 2026 composite is $0.4056/hr, of which $0.13195 is the employee deduction, so the employer's net is about $0.27/hr ($570/FTE/yr). PFML is 1.13% but the employer share applies only at 50+ employees; WA Cares (0.58%) is employee-paid. State minimum wage is $17.13 (2026) and $17.73 (2027); Bellevue has no local minimum, while Seattle ($21.30), Tukwila ($21.65) and Renton ($20.57-$21.57) do. The 2026 exempt-salary floor is $80,168.40, which constrains a salaried CAD/CAM director, and the noncompete floor is $126,858.83, so a sales rep's noncompete is unenforceable. Wages: BLS OEWS May 2025 (via BLS-derived mirrors) shows WA dental lab techs at $56,400 median ($27.12/hr), P10 $39,970 to P90 $78,340, with 670 jobs statewide and 380 in the Seattle MSA (median $57,610). Live King County postings cluster at $20-$25/hr entry (Novus, Nuvia), $22-$35 (MicroDental/DiMartino, Renton) and $25-$38 (Bellevue removables). Sales reps: posted structures are $60-$75k base plus 5-6% of net sales (DCS, MRM), $60k base plus $250-$750 per new account (Ziemek), or 100% commission at 8% year-one and 3% residual (Ultra Core). LMT's 40/60 fixed/commission guideline is 2011-vintage. Expect $85-$110k loaded year one for a Puget Sound rep, before any sales. Equipment list prices: Dekema 674i $14,582-$15,999; used Programat P710 $5,700-$6,000; Asiga MAX UV $8,990, MAX 2 UV $9,990-$10,990; a third ARUM mill $51,000 list (A3L) or $17,500 at demo/dealer cost, versus Versamill 5X200 at $103,600 with install/training. Marketing: LMT LAB DAY West (May 14-15, 2027) does not publish booth prices. The WSDLA/Oregon labs' Pacific Northwest Conference (2027 in Seattle) sells tables at $1,050-$1,250 and program sponsorship at $1,200. SKCDS and WSDA PNDC do not publish rates. WordStream's 2026 benchmark CPC for dentists is $8.00 (no dental-lab-specific CPC exists). Marketing Mail letters cost $0.339-$0.372 postage; small-run postcards cost $0.85-$1.50 all-in. Shipping: nearest UPS Store (14150 NE 20th St) Air cutoff 4:30 PM M-F; FedEx Office 40 Bellevue Way Express pickup 4:35 PM M-F; Crossroads/Factoria FedEx Office cutoffs unconfirmed. Seattle Messenger Co-op zone prices (from downtown): Bellevue $34.97, Tukwila $31.13, Renton $37.83, Redmond $43.12 standard, 1.5x priority, 2x rush, +20% fuel. ESTIMATES (labeled): lab build-out $10-60k; personal property tax ~$1.4-1.7k/yr; dental-lab Google CPC $3-$8; trade-show 10x10 $2.5-4k; dedicated courier route $12-35/stop. UNKNOWNS: LMT and PNDC booth prices, SUTA rate, Bellevue square-footage tax applicability to production floor, CAM license cost for a third mill, FedEx Ship Center late cutoff, and actual OEWS percentile table for the Seattle MSA (BLS pages blocked crawlers; mirrors used). Judgment: the binding constraints are not occupancy or tax. At $11,700/mo revenue the state B&O is already zero and L&I is a rounding error; adding 1,000-1,500 SF of flex space costs $1,900-$5,700/mo, which is 16-49% of current monthly collections, so it only makes sense after volume, not before. The single largest incremental cost of growing in place is a salesperson ($85-110k/yr loaded), and the mills' 90% idle time means no equipment spend is justified until utilization exceeds roughly 50%.

**Findings.**

- *fact, high confidence.* Seattle Exchange @ Southcenter (Tukwila) current asking rent is $2.10/SF/mo blended, modified gross, listed by Lee & Associates. Value: $2.10/SF/mo modified gross; 1,410 SF = $2,961/mo; 2,119 SF = $4,450/mo. [source](https://www.commercialmls.com/Search/ListingDetailsFull/710417) (2026-09-22)
- *fact, high confidence.* Seattle Exchange is running a promotional rate of $1.00/SF/mo for the first full year on eligible spaces. Value: $1.00/SF/mo year 1 promo. [source](https://www.commercialmls.com/Search/ListingDetailsFull/686672) (2026-08-14)
- *fact, high confidence.* Eastside industrial direct asking rent averaged $1.88/SF/mo NNN in Q2 2026 with 6.0% vacancy; Southend $1.01-$1.05 with 10.4% vacancy; regional 9.5%. Value: Eastside $1.88 NNN/mo; Southend $1.01-1.05; vacancy 6.0% / 10.4%. [source](https://kidder.com/wp-content/uploads/market_report/industrial-market-research-seattle-2026-2q.pdf) (2026 Q2 (July 2026))
- *fact, high confidence.* Cushman & Wakefield Q2 2026 Eastside weighted asking rents: Bellevue $1.58, Kirkland $1.86, Redmond $1.76-$1.89, Issaquah/East I-90 $1.51, Eastside overall $1.26 NNN/mo. Value: Bellevue $1.58; Kirkland $1.86; Redmond $1.76-1.89 ($/SF/mo NNN). [source](https://upgrade-uat-www.cushmanwakefield.com/-/media/cw/marketbeat-pdfs/2026/q2/us-reports/industrial/puget-sound-eastside_americas_marketbeat_industrial_q22026.pdf) (2026 Q2)
- *fact, high confidence.* Redmond Willows small flex asks $1.80-$2.04/SF/mo NNN, with 2026 NNN of $0.57/SF/mo at one park; short-term gross sublease bays at $2.40/SF/mo. Value: $1.80-2.04 NNN + $0.57 NNN; $2.40 gross sublease. [source](https://cbaimage.commercialmls.com/content/upload/photos/pdf/FL/6a7df26957d81058c977e74a_95th_street_tech_center_flyer.pdf) (2026 (flyer, undated day))
- *fact, medium confidence.* Renton/Tukwila all-inclusive small industrial bays (ReadySpaces) list at $27.60 and $25.80/SF/yr ($2.30 and $2.15/SF/mo) for 300-5,000 SF, 90-day minimum terms. Value: $2.15-2.30/SF/mo all-in; Kent $1.55 NNN + ~$0.42 NNN. [source](https://www.cityfeet.com/cont/tukwila-wa/industrial-space-for-lease) (undated (accessed 2026-10-12))
- *fact, high confidence.* Bellevue small office/retail suites near 148th Ave SE ask $20-$34/SF/yr plus $8-$15.38 NNN (about $2.58-$3.78/SF/mo all-in). Value: $2.58-3.78/SF/mo all-in. [source](https://cbaimage.commercialmls.com/content/upload/photos/pdf/FL/66f1c82d07ce72572499806b_1600_148th_ave_se_for_lease.pdf) (undated flyer; CBA records modified 2026-02-09 to 2026-09-30)
- *fact, high confidence.* Dental lab fabrication is a manufacturing activity for WA B&O; sales also taxed under retailing or wholesaling with the Multiple Activities Tax Credit; prostheses are sales-tax exempt. Value: Manufacturing + Retailing/Wholesaling with MATC. [source](https://app.leg.wa.gov/wac/default.aspx?cite=458-20-151) (undated (current WAC))
- *fact, high confidence.* 2026 WA B&O rates: Manufacturing 0.484%, Wholesaling 0.484%, Retailing 0.471%, Service & Other 1.5% (<$1M), 1.75% ($1M-$5M), 2.1% ($5M+). Value: 0.484% / 0.484% / 0.471% / 1.5%. [source](https://dor.wa.gov/taxes-rates/business-occupation-tax/business-occupation-tax-classifications) (undated (current DOR page))
- *fact, medium confidence.* Manufacturing, wholesaling and retailing B&O rates rise to 0.50% effective January 1, 2027 (ESHB 2081, 2025 session). Value: 0.50% from 2027-01-01. [source](https://www.bdo.com/insights/tax/washington-governor-signs-biennial-operating-budget-and-transportation-funding-bills) (2025-05)
- *fact, high confidence.* Small Business B&O Tax Credit: max $55/month for non-service filers ($165/qtr, $660/yr) and $160/month for service filers, fully offsetting B&O at or below the cap and phasing out at 2x the cap. Value: $55/mo (non-service); $160/mo (service). [source](https://dor.wa.gov/node/151433) (2022-12 (effective 2023-01-01))
- *fact, high confidence.* Dental prostheses sold to dentists are exempt from WA retail sales tax under RCW 82.08.0283 (prosthetic devices). Value: Exempt (0% vs 10.3% Bellevue rate). [source](https://app.leg.wa.gov/rcw/default.aspx?cite=82.08.0283) (undated (current RCW; last amended 2007))
- *fact, high confidence.* Mills, furnaces and printers used directly in manufacturing qualify for the WA manufacturer's machinery & equipment sales/use tax exemption (RCW 82.08.02565 / WAC 458-20-13601). Value: 0% sales tax on qualifying lab equipment (saves 10.3%). [source](https://app.leg.wa.gov/rcw/default.aspx?cite=82.08.02565) (undated (current RCW))
- *fact, high confidence.* City of Bellevue levies its own B&O tax: 0.1596% of gross receipts above $215,000 taxable receipts, a square footage tax of $0.3297475/sq ft/quarter on qualifying office/support space, and a $119 business license registration (2026). Value: 0.1596% above $215k; $0.3297475/sq ft/qtr; $119 license. [source](https://bellevuewa.gov/sites/default/files/media/pdf_document/2025/fam-2026-26745-botaxguide-v01.pdf) (2026-01-01)
- *fact, high confidence.* WA L&I risk class 3602 ('Electronic Products, Jewelry and Instrument Mfg, Dental Labs'; subclass 3602-05 dental laboratories) 2026 composite base rate is $0.4056 per hour worked, up 6% from $0.3826 in 2025. Value: $0.4056/hr composite; employer net ~$0.274/hr; employee $0.13195/hr. [source](https://lni.wa.gov/insurance/_docs/2026RatesByClassCodeFund.pdf) (2026 (adopted Nov 26, 2025))
- *fact, high confidence.* WA Paid Family & Medical Leave 2026 premium is 1.13% of wages (up from 0.92%), split 28.57% employer / 71.43% employee, but employers under 50 employees owe no employer share. Value: 1.13% total; employer share 0.323% only if 50+ employees; employee share 0.807%. [source](https://esd.wa.gov/node/1109) (2025-10-29)
- *fact, high confidence.* WA Cares Fund premium is 0.58% of gross wages, employee-paid, no wage cap, employer remits only. Value: 0.58% employee-paid. [source](https://wacaresfund.wa.gov/employers) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* WA minimum wage is $17.13/hr in 2026 and $17.73/hr in 2027; Bellevue, Kirkland and Redmond have no local minimum, while Seattle ($21.30), Tukwila ($21.65), Renton ($21.57 large / $20.57 then $21.57 mid-size from 7/1/2026), Burien and unincorporated King County ($18.32-$20.82) exceed it. Value: $17.13 (2026) / $17.73 (2027); Bellevue = state rate; Seattle $21.30; Tukwila $21.65. [source](https://lni.wa.gov/workers-rights/wages/minimum-wage/) (2026 (page shows 2026 and 2027 rates))
- *fact, medium confidence.* BLS OEWS May 2025: Washington dental laboratory technicians (51-9081) median $56,400/yr ($27.12/hr), mean $58,080, P10 $39,970, P25 $47,830, P75 $67,810, P90 $78,340, 670 jobs; Seattle-Tacoma-Bellevue MSA median $57,610 with 380 jobs. **Competitors mapped.**

- **MicroDental Laboratories / Modern Dental Lab USA (DiMartino Dental Lab)** (Renton, WA): National lab group's Renton center (fixed, implant, digital dentures); labor-market competitor for technicians Pricing: Posting pay $22-$35/hr denture technician (Aug 4 and Oct 7, 2026) Turnaround: n/a 25+ North American locations; competes for the same King County technician pool with benefits packages Integra cannot match. [source](https://jobs.smartrecruiters.com/moderndentallaboratories/744000141564853)
- **Paragon Dental Arts LLC** (Renton, WA): Independent full-service lab (model, wax/metal, zirconia fitting, ceramist) Pricing: Posting pay from $25/hr, full-time Turnaround: n/a Direct labor-market comparator for Integra's technician wage setting. [source](https://www.dentalofficejobs.com/jobs/473666754-dental-lab-technician-cdt)
- **Novus Dental Lab** (Bellevue, WA): Bellevue crown & bridge lab hiring model-work technician with CAD familiarity Pricing: Posting pay from $20/hr Turnaround: n/a Same city as Integra; sets the entry-level floor locally. [source](https://www.dentalofficejobs.com/jobs/422914658-dental-lab-technician-model-work)
- **Nuvia Dental Implant Center (in-house lab)** (Bellevue, WA): DSO-style full-arch implant center with in-house Bellevue lab Pricing: Entry articulator $20/hr training, $25/hr after (posted Sept 17, 2026) Turnaround: n/a Illustrates that full-arch providers in Bellevue internalize lab work; both a labor competitor and a signal that large full-arch accounts are not available to outside labs. [source](https://www.jobsparx.com/Job/B14641829/Dr-Blair-Alexander-Isom-Pllc-Entry-Level-Bilingual-Dental-Lab-Technician-Articulator-Bellevue-WA)
- **Shama Dental Lab** (Seattle, WA): Seattle lab hiring part-time technician who scans cases into design software Pricing: $22-$30/hr part-time Turnaround: n/a Comparable small digital lab; pay range brackets Integra's likely part-time offers. [source](https://www.ziprecruiter.com/Jobs/Dental-Laboratory-Technician/-in-Seattle,WA)

**Unknowns the agent could not resolve.**

- LMT LAB DAY West 2027 booth price (not published; request from Tiffany Hurley, tiffany@lmtmag.com; 2026 booking deadline was Nov 24, 2025, so 2027 deadline likely ~Nov 2026).

- Whether Bellevue's square footage B&O tax ($0.3297475/sq ft/qtr) applies to Integra's production floor as 'support space' for out-of-city sales (lab-to-lab shipments to NY/CA/OR/HI), and whether it is owed while below the $215,000 gross-receipts threshold. Confirm with tax@bellevuewa.gov.
- Washington unemployment insurance (ESD) rate and 2026 taxable wage base for Integra's account (estimated ~1.0-1.5% on first ~$78k; see rate notice).
- King County 2026 personal property tax levy rate applicable to the Bellevue lab (estimated $8-$10 per $1,000 assessed).
- CAM license cost (hyperDENT or MillBox seat/post-processor) for a third mill; not sourced.
- FedEx Express cutoff times at FedEx Office 1299 156th Ave NE (Crossroads) and 3632 Factoria Blvd SE, and the latest Express tender time at FedEx Ship Center 651 S Alaska St, Seattle ('extended West Coast service'); only store hours (M-F 8 AM-7 PM) were retrievable. Call 425-957-4415 / 425-641-1174.
- Eastside-origin (Bellevue pickup) courier zone pricing; Seattle Messenger Cooperative publishes downtown-Seattle-origin zones only; PNW Couriers, AMCO, Cordova and DAGO are quote-based.
- Actual NNN/CAM for a specific Tukwila or Redmond suite (modified-gross vs NNN terms vary by building; observed range $0.15/SF/mo CAM at Seattle Exchange to $0.57/SF/mo NNN at 95th Street Tech Center).
- Public US list price for ARUM 5X-200 / A3L (dealer-confidential; only Blue Sky Bio's $103,600 turnkey Versamill 5X200 and an Australian AUD $167,000 package are public).
- Dentist/practice counts in King County and Bellevue for sizing direct-mail drops (ADA HPI/WA DOH license counts not pulled in this pass).

### B.3 South Orange County market

*Scope:* South Orange County, CA (Aliso Viejo, Laguna Niguel, Laguna Beach, Laguna Hills, Mission Viejo, Dana Point, San Juan Capistrano, San Clemente, Irvine, Newport Beach) dental-lab market assessment for Integra Dental Design / Bear Milling

**Agent summary.** Bottom line: South Orange County is one of the most lab-saturated dental markets in the United States, and nothing found in this pass supports calling it underserved. The county contains the world's largest dental lab (Glidewell: ~1M sq ft in Irvine/Newport Beach, ~5,000 employees, 100,000+ units/week, 24,000 dentists sending work weekly, 3-day BruxZir, $9 FedEx overnight each way), the Irvine support center of the third-largest DSO (PDS Health, 1,381 OC employees, nearly every supported office running CEREC), Smile Brands' HQ, Western Dental's HQ, and a dense tail of independent labs in Irvine, Santa Ana, Anaheim, Garden Grove and Westminster. The most lucrative south-OC full-arch practices (Wöhrle, Providence Prosthodontics, Laguna Hills Prosthodontics, Aliso Viejo Family Dental, 4M, New Life, Same Day Smile Designs) already own in-house labs or mills. Facts (sourced): Orange County Dental Society reports 2,500+ active members and is California's largest component (ocds.org, undated; third-party figure 2,530 at Jan 2025). UCLA CHPR's 2021 table puts Orange County at 5.3 dentists per 5,000 residents (2020 Dental Board data), which on ~3.17M residents implies roughly 3,300-3,400 licensed dentists; a directory lists 3,998. King County baseline: Seattle-King County Dental Society has ~1,476-1,700 members and UW CHWS reports 112 licensed dentists per 100,000 in King County (2023). State-level Census CBP (via a secondary compiler): California 734 dental-lab establishments vs Washington 175. Glidewell's published prices: BruxZir Full-Strength $109 ($89 digital model-free), screw-retained implant crown $339 (7 days in lab), BruxZir full-arch implant prosthesis $3,433 (Glidewell-affiliate price lists). Jim Glidewell (2022 interview): "the average laboratory crown in the United States is $150... we're at $99," ~$79-80 model-free. LMT LAB DAY West 2026 drew 1,600+ attendees and 131 exhibitors in Garden Grove (May 15-16, 2026); 2027 is May 14-15 at the same Hyatt Regency. TRI Dental Implants USA is registered in Dover, DE but its US support (949-676-7383) and lab (949-210-3877) lines are Orange County numbers, its Area Sales Director is based in Irvine, and it runs study clubs in Irvine; no OC street address was found. Estimates (labeled): County-level CBP NAICS 339116 counts could not be retrieved (API key required; data.census.gov is script-rendered; curl blocked). Using state CBP totals and directory shares, Orange County plausibly has ~90-120 payroll lab establishments plus Glidewell's ~1,400-1,500 OC lab employees, versus ~55-70 in King County. That is roughly one independent lab per 30 dentists in OC versus one per 40 in King County, before counting Glidewell, so lab supply per dentist in OC is higher, not lower, than Integra's home market. Scraped directory listings (April 2026) show ~330 "dental laboratory" listings across OC cities (Anaheim 49, Irvine 31, Buena Park 25, Orange 24, Garden Grove 22, Santa Ana 21...), inflated by in-office labs and misclassification. The 10 target cities hold roughly 25% of OC population, implying ~800-1,000 dentists (estimate). In-office milling: one query surfaced ~10 independent CEREC/E4D/Roland practices in the target cities plus every PDS-supported office; PDS itself cites <10% national chairside-CAD/CAM penetration, so 10-20% of south-OC GP practices is a reasonable estimate. Pricing comparison: Integra's $129 full-contour zirconia is $20-$40 above Glidewell and at the top of local independent lists ($85-$125). e.max at $149 is parity with Glidewell. Integra's implant crown package ($269-$299) undercuts Glidewell's $339 screw-retained and $432-$454 custom-abutment packages. Integra's $1,700 full-arch zirconia and $2,200 ti-bar undercut Glidewell ($3,433) and ADL ($3,750) but an Irvine lab (Avant Dental Arts) advertises full zirconia hybrid arches "from $899" on sale (list $3,450), and lab-to-lab price lists show full-arch zirconia at $999-$1,999 and FCZ at $39-$59, so Bear Milling's $22 mill-only and $650 full-arch are price-competitive only at the wholesale tier. Turnaround and shipping: Glidewell sets expectations at 3 days in lab (next-day available for digital cases), $9 FedEx overnight each way or free USPS Priority, plus scheduled in-office pickup. Local labs advertise 3-5 day turnaround, courier pickup (OD's, IDOC), and one Laguna Hills lab offers 4-hour same-day zirconia within 10 miles. A Bellevue lab with 3 days in-lab plus two shipping legs lands at 5-8 calendar days door-to-door for physical cases, slower than the local norm; digital-impression cases remove the inbound leg. Reach channels: OCDS (Orange, CA), West Coast Study Club (Seattle Study Club affiliate, co-directors Drs. Jin Kim and Gianmarco O'Brien), Dental Implants GPS Full-Arch Study Club (South OC/North OC, quarterly, free), "Wednesdays with the Specialists" held at Keating Dental Lab in Irvine, Glidewell Study Club ($495/yr), Osseointegration Study Club of Southern California, Implant Study Group of Southern California, TRI USA Irvine study clubs, and LAB DAY West. Harbor Dental Society covers Long Beach/South Bay plus only the northwest OC fringe (Seal Beach, Los Alamitos, Rossmoor, Surfside), not south OC. Honest switching assessment: A dentist in Laguna Niguel has Glidewell 20 minutes away, a dozen independent labs offering courier pickup within 15 miles, and probably a CEREC down the hall. The reasons to try a Bellevue lab reduce to (1) a personal relationship, (2) an implant-crown and full-arch price that beats Glidewell by $40-$1,700, (3) a TRI matrix certification niche once completed, and (4) equipment/lab-to-lab selling to OC's small labs and in-office millers, which is a different business than selling crowns to dentists. Winning dentist accounts without a local pickup/shade/try-in presence is unlikely; the local-office idea only works if it buys courier-level service, and that cost was not researched here.

**Findings.**

- *fact, high confidence.* Orange County Dental Society membership and scope. Value: 2,500+ active members; 50+ new members/yr; largest of California's 32 components; 10% of CDA members. [source](https://ocds.org/about-us/) (undated (accessed 2026-10-12))
- *fact, high confidence.* Dentists per capita, Orange County. Value: 5.3 dentists per 5,000 population (≈1 per 943 residents). [source](https://healthpolicy.ucla.edu/publications/Documents/PDF/2021/Table-DentistsCACounty-jun2021.pdf) (June 2021 (accessed 2026-10-12))
- *estimate, medium confidence.* Alternative OC dentist counts. Value: ~2,460 (Dentx population-share estimate, 2026-06-08); 3,998 (FindClarity directory, undated). [source](https://dentx.ca/blog/orange-county-dental-market-report/) (2026-06-08 (accessed 2026-10-12))
- *estimate, low confidence.* Dentists in the 10 target south-OC cities. Value: ≈800-1,000 practicing dentists (estimate). [source](https://healthpolicy.ucla.edu/publications/Documents/PDF/2021/Table-DentistsCACounty-jun2021.pdf) (June 2021 (accessed 2026-10-12))
- *fact, medium confidence.* King County, WA dentist baseline. Value: SKCDS ≈1,476 members (May 31, 2024, Form 990); 'almost 1,700' per website; 112 licensed dentists per 100,000 in King County (2023, UW CHWS). [source](https://familymedicine.uw.edu/chws/resources/wohw/workforce-data/current-supply/) (undated page citing 2023 data (accessed 2026-10-12); SKCDS 990 via https://siviq.co/nonprofits/organizations/910520407/seattle-king-county-dental-society/returns/202530729349300133/2024)
- *unknown, medium confidence.* Census CBP NAICS 339116 county counts for Orange County CA and King County WA could not be retrieved. Value: unresolved. [source](https://www.fairmarketvalue.com/resources/industries/339116/dental-laboratories) (undated (accessed 2026-10-12))
- *estimate, low confidence.* Estimated lab establishments: Orange County vs King County. Value: OC ≈90-120 payroll labs (plus Glidewell ≈1,400-1,500 OC lab employees); King County ≈55-70 payroll labs. [source](https://rentechdigital.com/smartscraper/business-report-details/united-states/list-of-dental-laboratories-in-california) (2026-04-01 (accessed 2026-10-12))
- *fact, high confidence.* Glidewell scale and location. Value: ~1,000,000 sq ft Irvine/Newport Beach campus; ~5,000 employees (1,381 in OC per OCBJ Aug 2026 list); 100,000+ units/week; 24,000 dentists send work every week, ~70,000/yr. [source](https://glidewelldental.com/company/blog/glidewell-advantage-pillars-peerless-scale) (2025-08-12 (accessed 2026-10-12); volume metrics from https://www.groupdentistrynow.com/dso-group-blog/the-group-dentistry-now-show-the-voice-of-the-dso-industry-episode-74/ (2022-01-05); OC headcount from https://www.ocbj.com/healthcare/pds-health-becomes-ocs-largest-dental-co/ (2026-08-03))
- *fact, high confidence.* Glidewell published crown prices and turnaround. Value: BruxZir Full-Strength $109 ($89 model-free digital); BruxZir Esthetic $119 ($99); BruxZir Radiant $149 ($129); 3 days in lab; Next-Day BruxZir for digital cases (scan by 2 p.m., receive 10:30 a.m. next day, up to 3 units). [source](https://glidewelldental.com/solutions/crown-and-bridge/zirconia/bruxzir-full-strength-solid-zirconia) (page dated 2019-09-08, prices current on page (accessed 2026-10-12); digital prices confirmed in https://glidewelldental.com/company/blog/bruxzir-zirconia-restorative-dentistrys-recession-buster (2024-12-10))
- *fact, high confidence.* Glidewell implant and full-arch prices. Value: Screw-retained crown (Ti-base) $339, 7 days in lab; BruxZir crown over custom abutment $432-$454 (13 days); BruxZir full-arch implant prosthesis $3,433 incl. up to 6 MUAs and two try-ins; screw-retained hybrid with milled Ti bar $2,335. [source](https://glidewelldental.com/solutions/implant-solutions/implant-restorations/screw-retained-crowns) (page dated 2019-09-05, prices current (accessed 2026-10-12); full-arch from https://riversidelab.com/price-list/ (undated) and https://crownworlddental.com/lab-services-2025/ (2025-09-10))
- *fact, high confidence.* How dentists ship cases to the dominant local lab. Value: FedEx Overnight $9 each way ($18 round trip) using Glidewell-preprinted labels; prepaid USPS Priority labels free (2-4 business days each way); scheduled in-office pickup; bundling multiple cases per box. [source](https://glidewelldental.com/send-case/pickup-and-delivery) (2026-05-05 (accessed 2026-10-12))
- *fact, high confidence.* Local independent lab turnaround norms in OC. Value: 3 days (Creative Image, Laguna Hills; 38 Smiles, Fountain Valley); 3-5 business days (IDOC); 5 working days model-free zirconia (Artidental, Irvine); 12 business days (Pro Arts, City of Industry); same-day 4-hour posterior zirconia within 10 miles (Creative Image). [source](https://creativeimagedentallab.com/) (undated (accessed 2026-10-12); IDOC https://www.idocdentallab.com/; 38 Smiles https://38smileslab.com/; Artidental https://www.artidentallab.com/)

- *fact, medium confidence.* National labs serving OC and whether they have local facilities. Value: Glidewell: local (Irvine/Newport Beach). **Competitors mapped.**

- **Glidewell** (4141 MacArthur Blvd, Newport Beach 92660 (HQ); 2201 Dupont Dr, Irvine 92612 (campus); ~1M sq ft): World's largest dental lab; full-service crown/bridge, implants, full-arch, removables; also chairside mills (glidewell.io/fastmill.io), BruxZir material sales to other labs Pricing: BruxZir Full-Strength $109 / $89 digital; Esthetic $119/$99; Radiant $149/$129; e.max ~$149; screw-retained implant crown $339; crown + custom abutment $432-$454; BruxZir full-arch implant prosthesis $3,433; Ti-bar hybrid $2,335; 7-yr no-fault warranty Turnaround: 3 days in lab (all BruxZir); Next-Day BruxZir for digital ≤3 units; implants 7 days; packages 13 days; FedEx overnight $9 each way or free USPS; scheduled pickup ~5,000 employees (1,381 in OC), 100,000+ units/week, 24,000 dentists/week. Runs Glidewell Study Club ($495/yr) and education center in Irvine. Price and speed anchor for the entire market. [source](https://glidewelldental.com/solutions/crown-and-bridge/zirconia/bruxzir-full-strength-solid-zirconia)
- **Keating Dental Lab** (16881 Hale Ave, Irvine, CA 92606): Large full-service lab; KDZ Bruxer zirconia, e.max, implants (Atlantis/Nobel/custom), full-arch hybrids, digital dentures; accepts TRIOS/iTero/CEREC/Medit Pricing: Not published on fetched pages Turnaround: Not published on fetched pages Founded 2002 by Shaun Keating CDT; serves 'thousands of practices across the country'; hosts the monthly 'Wednesdays with the Specialists' OC study club on-site, embedding itself with Irvine specialists. - Census CBP NAICS 339116 establishment and employee counts for Orange County CA (FIPS 06059) and King County WA (53033): not retrievable here (API key required; data.census.gov script-rendered; BLS QCEW CSV and curl blocked). Needed to replace the ~90-120 vs ~55-70 estimate.
- Exact number of practicing/licensed dentists in the ten target cities; only county-level ratios exist (UCLA 5.3/5,000; OCDS 2,500+ members).
- TRI Dental Implants USA physical office address in Orange County (only 949 phone lines, an Irvine-based Area Sales Director and Irvine events were found; registered address is Dover, DE).
- Whether PDS Health, Smile Brands or Western Dental operate captive dental laboratories in Orange County.
- Which OC independent labs own 5-axis (especially wet) mills; mill brands are unpublished for nearly all labs listed.
- Current operating status of several small directory-listed south-OC labs (Sowle Ceramics, Andre Dental Studio, Okon, SMT Dental Technologies, Tapie, Ippin, Pacific Edge, Gnathic Concepts).
- IDOC Dental Lab street address (claims OC local pickup); PRO-Craft Dental Laboratory location (markets to OC); Mabel, ADL and TDL lab locations (used as price benchmarks).
- Street address for Dr. Heldt's Newport Beach implant practice and for Niguel Coast Oral & Facial Surgery.
- Actual CEREC/in-office mill penetration rate among south-OC practices (estimate 10-20%); no Dentsply Sirona locator data retrieved.
- Retail FedEx/UPS cost and transit for Bellevue→south OC small-box dental cases (estimate $20-$45 each way, 2 business days ground).
- LMT LAB DAY West exhibitor/tabletop pricing and OCDS vendor/affiliate program costs.
- Cost of a small south-OC office or courier arrangement (out of scope for this market pass; must be nonzero in any model).
- Any public complaints or documented dissatisfaction by south-OC dentists with local labs: none found; do not assume it.

### B.4 South Orange County costs and California compliance

*Scope:* All-in cost of a South Orange County, CA presence (Aliso Viejo / Laguna Niguel / Laguna Hills / Mission Viejo / Laguna Beach) for Integra Dental Design: footprints A (150-400 sf office), B (600-1,200 sf flex finishing/QC satellite), C (1,200-2,000 sf production lab), plus California tax, licensing, labor, insurance, utility and travel costs. Desk research, accessed 2026-10-12.

**Agent summary.** Bottom line: small space in South Orange County is findable and not expensive at the unit level; the California compliance and labor stack, plus the fact that a leased satellite by itself drags the whole WA LLC into California tax jurisdiction, is where the cost sits. Against Integra's reconciled ~$11,700/month of collections, a staffed Footprint B runs roughly $6,500-7,000/month all-in (about 55-60% of current collections) and Footprint C roughly $10,000-11,000/month before materials. Footprint A is roughly $2,200-2,500/month including one monthly trip. Note: WebSearch budget was exhausted at the start of this task; all discovery ran through Exa search/fetch. LoopNet detail pages were readable via Exa search snippets but not by direct fetch. FACTS (primary sources, accessed 2026-10-12). Space: live listings include 400-460 sf second-floor offices at 27992/28052 Camino Capistrano, Laguna Niguel at $700/month Modified Gross (updated Jan 20, 2026) and a 1,600 sf flex unit in the same park at $2,800/month MG; 1,200 sf flex at 23372 Madero, Mission Viejo at $1.75/sf/mo (utilities and property expenses excluded); 1,768 sf flex at Saddleback Business Park, 27071 Cabot Rd, Laguna Hills at $2.00/sf/mo with a fixed $0.35-0.36 CAM and 100-200A three-phase power; 1,920-1,960 sf at 23322 Madero at $1.75; 2,800 sf NNN at 23561 Ridge Route at $1.70/sf/mo (updated Sep 26, 2026); 809 sf full-service office at 27141 Aliso Creek Rd at $2.45/sf/mo; 207-402 sf suites at 85 Argonaut, Aliso Viejo at $41.76-57.96/sf/yr. Market context: Kidder Mathews 3Q 2026 puts South County average direct asking industrial rent at $1.71 NNN (Laguna Hills/Aliso Viejo $1.67 at 2.8% vacancy; Mission Viejo $1.77 at 0.7% vacancy; Laguna Niguel/Laguna Beach has only 291,371 sf of industrial inventory in total), so sub-2,000 sf units near Aliso Viejo are scarce rather than cheap. Executive-suite benchmark: Regus 15 Enterprise, Aliso Viejo private office from $519/month on a 1-month minimum. Taxes: CDTFA Regulation 1506(e) makes dental labs retailers taxed on the entire charge to dentists; Regulation 1591(b)(5) explicitly lists crowns, bridges, dentures, inlays as not medicines (taxable), while 1591(b)(2) lists dental implant systems including bone screws and abutments as exempt medicines when sold to a dentist for treatment, so separately stated abutment charges can be exempt. The combined rate in Aliso Viejo, Laguna Beach, Laguna Hills, Laguna Niguel and Mission Viejo is 7.75% (effective July 1, 2026). A CA office is physical presence requiring CDTFA registration for all CA sales; without it the remote-seller threshold is $500,000 of CA sales (RTC 6203, AB 147). FTB: Form LLC-5 registration fee is $70; the $800 annual LLC tax applies to any LLC registered or doing business in CA (first-year waiver ended for years starting on/after Jan 1, 2024); the gross-receipts LLC fee starts at $900 only above $250,000 of CA income. FTB "doing business" thresholds for 2025 are $757,070 sales, $75,707 property, $75,707 payroll. Payroll: 2026 CA UI new-employer rate 3.4% on $7,000, ETT 0.1% on $7,000, SDI 1.3% employee-paid with no wage cap; minimum wage $16.90; EDD projects 2026 FUTA at 2.1% ($147/employee) because California is a credit-reduction state. Workers' comp class 4692 (Dental Laboratories) advisory pure premium is $1.48 per $100 effective Sep 1, 2026 (secondary source citing WCIRB; CDI approved an average benchmark of $1.65). Wages: EDD/OEWS for the Anaheim-Santa Ana-Irvine MD (2026 Q1) show dental lab technicians at a $21.89 median, $18.02 25th percentile, $29.17 75th percentile; Orange County employment is about 1,230 with flat 10-year projections. Current Irvine postings: $18-22/hour entry (Glidewell, Ultimate Staffing, Simply Biotech, Aug-Oct 2026); Keating Dental Lab $40-65k for experienced techs. Licensing: Aliso Viejo, Laguna Niguel, Laguna Hills and Mission Viejo have no business license tax; each requires a certificate of occupancy/business registration (Laguna Hills COUO $250.10). Laguna Beach taxes gross receipts ($60 base + 0.00044-0.00088 per dollar, $1,650 cap) plus a $414 use-and-occupancy fee. California has no dental-lab registration statute; labs operate under the BPC 1626(e) exemption requiring a dentist's signed written authorization, and 17 CCR 10446 exempts labs from device-manufacturer licensing. Travel: Alaska flies 6-7 daily nonstops SEA-SNA; Saver one-ways were $89-149 for November and $279-293 for mid-October departures on 2026-10-12; IRS mileage is 76 cents (Jul-Dec 2026). ESTIMATES (labeled): build-out for a furnace/compressor/ventilation satellite $8,000-60,000 (base $25,000) using OC contractor benchmarks of $12-35/sf for TI electrical and $8,000-30,000 for panel capacity; NNN add-ons $0.35-0.65/sf/mo from three disclosed South OC pass-throughs; security deposit 1-3 months (no statutory cap under Civil Code 1950.7; a new foreign LLC should expect the high end or a guaranty); electricity 23-31 cents/kWh (EIA CA commercial averages) giving $200-500/month for B and $400-1,000 for C; liability insurance $1,500-3,500/yr bundled for a small lab per a specialty broker, full package $3,000-7,000; employer payroll load 11-15% of wages for a part-time tech including WC. UNKNOWNS: whether a dental lab is permitted by right in the specific business-park zoning (Aliso Viejo instructs tenants to verify before signing); Laguna Niguel and Mission Viejo C of O fees and Aliso Viejo's business-registration invoice amount; carrier WC minimum premium; sales-tax treatment of screw-retained titanium bars; actual insurance quotes; CalSavers and OCFA hazardous-materials triggers; landlord appetite for a 1-year or month-to-month term for a WA entity. A separate consequence worth flagging: Oregon HB 2594 (effective July 1, 2026) requires out-of-state labs serving Oregon dentists to register with Oregon's HLO and provide material disclosures, which touches the Harmony Dental (Eugene) account regardless of the California decision.

**Findings.**

- *fact, high confidence.* Dental laboratories are retailers in California; sales tax applies to the lab's entire charge to a dentist. Value: 100% of lab charge taxable. [source](https://www.cdtfa.ca.gov/lawguides/vol1/sutr/1506.html) (Business Taxes Law Guide Revision 2025 (undated page))
- *fact, high confidence.* Crowns, bridges, dentures and inlays are explicitly not 'medicines'; dental implant systems including abutments are exempt medicines when sold to a dentist for treatment. Value: Crown taxable; abutment exempt if separately stated and sold to dentist. [source](https://www.cdtfa.ca.gov/lawguides/vol1/sutr/1591.html) (Business Taxes Law Guide Revision 2026 (undated page))
- *fact, high confidence.* Combined sales/use tax rate is 7.75% in Aliso Viejo, Laguna Beach, Laguna Hills, Laguna Niguel and Mission Viejo. Value: 7.75%. [source](https://www.cdtfa.ca.gov/taxes-and-fees/rates.aspx) (effective July 1, 2026)
- *fact, high confidence.* A California office or representatives create sales-tax nexus for the whole company; without physical presence the remote-seller threshold is $500,000 of CA sales. Value: $500,000 remote threshold; $0 threshold with physical presence. [source](https://www.cdtfa.ca.gov/industry/wayfair.htm) (undated (AB 147 effective April 25, 2019))
- *fact, high confidence.* Foreign LLC registration (Form LLC-5) filing fee is $70. Value: $70 one-time (+$20 SOI). [source](https://bpd.cdn.sos.ca.gov/llc/forms/llc-5.pdf) (Form rev. 11/2023)
- *fact, high confidence.* Every LLC registered or doing business in California owes the $800 annual tax; the gross-receipts LLC fee begins at $250,000 of California income. Value: $800/yr + $0 fee below $250k CA income. [source](https://www.ftb.ca.gov/file/business/types/limited-liability-company/index.html) (Last updated 03/05/2026)
- *fact, medium confidence.* A leased CA satellite with a part-time employee will likely make the WA LLC 'doing business' in California on the property or payroll test even before registration. Value: Property threshold $75,707 (2025). [source](https://www.ftb.ca.gov/file/business/doing-business-in-california.html) (Last updated 11/07/2025)
- *fact, high confidence.* 2026 California employer payroll taxes: UI 3.4% new-employer rate on $7,000; ETT 0.1% on $7,000; SDI 1.3% employee-paid with no wage cap. Value: UI $238 + ETT $7 per employee per year. [source](https://edd.ca.gov/en/payroll_taxes/rates_and_withholding/) (2026 rates (undated page))
- *fact, high confidence.* California employers face an elevated FUTA rate: 1.8% final for 2025 and a projected 2.1% for 2026 ($147 per employee) because of the state's outstanding federal UI loan. Value: $126 (2025 actual) to $147 (2026 projected) per employee; worst case $413. [source](https://edd.ca.gov/siteassets/files/pdf_pub_ctr/de202.pdf) (Rev. 15 (1-26))
- *fact, medium confidence.* Workers' comp class 4692 Dental Laboratories advisory pure premium is $1.48 per $100 of payroll effective Sep 1, 2026; carriers typically load 25-50% on top. Value: $1.48 pure premium; est. $1.85-2.25 loaded. [source](https://brightcoastins.com/workers-comp-codes/ca/4692) (effective 2026-09-01)
- *fact, high confidence.* California minimum wage is $16.90/hour effective January 1, 2026. Value: $16.90/hr. [source](https://www.dir.ca.gov/dlse/faq_minimumwage.htm) (effective January 1, 2026)
- *fact, high confidence.* Orange County (Anaheim-Santa Ana-Irvine MD) dental lab technician wages: mean $23.47/hr, median $21.89, 25th pct $18.02, 75th pct $29.17 (2026 Q1); ~1,230 employed, flat 10-year outlook. Value: Median $21.89/hr; 75th pct $29.17/hr. [source](https://labormarketinfo.edd.ca.gov/cgi/databrowsing/occExplorerQSDetails.asp?menuchoice=localAreaPro&soccode=519081++++&geogArea=0604000059) (2026 1st Qtr)
- *fact, high confidence.* Current Orange County dental-lab job postings pay $18-22/hour for entry-level production and $40,000-65,000/year for experienced bench technicians. Value: $18-22/hr entry; $19-31/hr experienced. [source](https://www.keatingdentallab.com/employment-application/) (Aug-Oct 2026 postings)
- *fact, high confidence.* Aliso Viejo has no business license program but requires a Business Registration (Form PS-250) with floor plan and Planning zoning clearance before operating; TI and signage need separate building permits. Value: No license tax; registration fee not published. [source](https://avcity.org/442/Business-Registration) (undated)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Aliso Viejo Planning fee schedule FY 2026-27: Sign Permit $182.55, Temporary Sign/Banner $49.15, Zoning/Occupancy Verification Letter $266.80, Administrative Use Permit/Minor CUP $1,095.28, Temporary Use Permit $751.25; building permit for a $5,000-6,000 TI valuation totals $389.30. Value: Sign permit $182.55; TI building permit ~$135-$450. [source](https://avcity.org/DocumentCenter/View/1259/Planning-Deposit--Fee-Schedule) (FY 2026-27)
- *fact, high confidence.* Laguna Niguel has no business license; a Certificate of Use and Occupancy with Building and OCFA inspection is required for every business. Value: $0 license; C of O fee unpublished. [source](https://www.cityoflagunaniguel.org/1810/No-Business-License) (handout dated 01/03/17; web page undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* Laguna Hills has no business license; Certificate of Use and Occupancy fee is $250.10. Value: $250.10 one-time. [source](https://lagunahillsca.gov/597/Certificate-of-Use-and-Occupancy-COUO) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* Mission Viejo has no business license fee or gross receipts tax; C of O required; planning Sign Review $1,200, Temporary Banner $80, Zoning Letter $175, CUP $3,500. Value: $0 license; sign review $1,200. [source](https://www.cityofmissionviejo.org/file/27660/download?token=Fl9eL8Ju) (Adopted fees effective 7/1/2025)
- *fact, high confidence.* Laguna Beach levies a gross-receipts business tax ($60 base plus 0.00044-0.00088 per dollar, $1,650 cap) plus a $414 Use and Occupancy fee, $20 application fee and $4 CASp fee. Value: ~$180-250/yr at $140k receipts + $414 one-time. [source](https://ecode360.com/42884859) (undated code page; form undated)
- *fact, medium confidence.* California does not register or license dental laboratories; labs operate under the Dental Practice Act exemption requiring a dentist's signed written authorization with each order, and are exempt from state device-manufacturer licensing. Value: No registration fee; written authorization required. [source](https://law.justia.com/codes/california/code-bpc/division-2/chapter-4/article-2/section-1626/) (2024 code edition)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Oregon HB 2594 (effective July 1, 2026) requires dental labs located outside Oregon that do business with Oregon dentists to register with the Oregon Health Licensing Office and provide material-content disclosures. Value: Registration required from July 1, 2026. [source](https://www.nadl.org/index.php?day=29&id=25%3Aoregon-becomes-the-latest-state-to-incorporate-nadl-s-model-bill-for-registration-education-and-disclosure&month=05&option=com_dailyplanetblog&view=entry&year=2025) (May 29, 2025)
- *fact, high confidence.* Footprint A listing: 27992/28052 Camino Capistrano, Laguna Niguel 92677 - 400 sf and 460 sf second-floor offices at $700/month, Modified Gross, available immediately. **Competitors mapped.**

- **Glidewell Dental (Glidewell Laboratories)** (Irvine, CA (4141 MacArthur Blvd and Newport/Alton campuses)): Largest US dental lab; Irvine HQ; sets the entry-level wage floor in Orange County ($18-19/hr) and offers benefits a micro-satellite cannot match Pricing: Not researched (direct-to-dentist national price list); labor postings $18.00-19.00/hr Turnaround: Not researched Postings dated Sep 10-15, 2026: Abutment Milling Technician $19/hr, Digital Order Prep $18/hr, CAM Print Technician $18/hr; indicates abundant entry-level labor but strong competition for skilled CAD/CAM designers [source](https://jobs-in.us/milling-technician_irvine-c426358/2026-09-glidewell-dental_i4200736051)
- **Keating Dental Lab** (16881 Hale Ave, Irvine, CA 92606): Full-service digitally equipped family-owned lab, ~20 minutes north of Aliso Viejo Pricing: Not researched; technician pay $40,000-65,000/yr; shipping clerk $18-21/hr Turnaround: Not researched Careers page (undated) shows experienced metal/wax and removables technicians at $40k-65k; useful wage comps for a staffed satellite [source](https://www.keatingdentallab.com/employment-application/)
