**Unknowns the agent could not resolve.**

- Whether a dental laboratory (light manufacturing with a furnace and compressed air) is permitted by right in the specific zoning of each candidate business park; Aliso Viejo requires Planning verification before lease signing and may require an Administrative Use Permit/minor CUP ($1,095.28) - not resolved by desk research
- Exact Aliso Viejo Business Registration invoice amount, Laguna Niguel Certificate of Use and Occupancy fee, and Mission Viejo Certificate of Occupancy fee (ranges estimated from neighboring cities)
- Whether screw-retained titanium bars/superstructures are 'permanently implanted' medicines under Regulation 1591(b)(2) (exempt) or taxable prosthetic devices under 1591(b)(5); CDTFA written advice (Publication 8) recommended before pricing Bear Milling/implant lines into CA
- Workers' compensation carrier minimum premium for a single part-time employee in class 4692; and whether California's 2026 FUTA credit reduction will be 1.5% or 5.3% (final after Nov 10, 2026)
- Actual insurance quotes for a California dental lab (GL/product/professional/BPP); figures above are broker-published ranges, not quotes
- Landlord willingness to lease to a newly registered Washington LLC on a 1-year or month-to-month term without a 2-3 month deposit or personal guaranty; Olen/Birtcher listings show 2-5 year or 3-year terms
- Lease type and minimum term for 85 Argonaut 'Ready Suites' (207-402 sf) and 26071 Merit Cir; whether 26800 Aliso Viejo Pkwy ($15/sf/yr, 500 sf min) is still available (LoopNet shows it no longer advertised)
- Whether lab chemical/dust quantities trigger OCFA hazardous-materials review or CUPA Hazardous Materials Business Plan filing; Laguna Niguel C of O application asks explicitly
- CalSavers registration obligation for a 1-employee CA worksite and any local (city) minimum wage above $16.90 (none identified in the five cities)
- Whether the FTB will treat the WA LLC as 'doing business' from the lease alone (8x rent property test) before any CA sales; confirm with CPA; Public Law 86-272 does not protect a company with in-state fabrication/finishing
- Actual Bear Milling lab-to-lab sales to California labs would be exempt sales for resale only with valid CDTFA resale certificates (Regulation 1668) - not verified against CDTFA for this fact pattern
- Oregon HB 2594 registration requirements and fees for out-of-state labs serving Oregon dentists (effective July 1, 2026) - applies to the Harmony Dental account; HLO fee schedule not researched
- WebSearch quota was exhausted for this task; discovery relied on Exa search/fetch only, so some listing sites (LoopNet direct pages, Crexi filters, Craigslist) could not be browsed exhaustively

### B.5 Maui and Hawaii market

*Scope:* Maui and Hawaii dental-lab market: verify/refute "Maui lacks dental labs"; dentist counts; current lab supply chain; Maui-specific tax, registration, licensing, wage, rent and travel economics; obtainable demand for a Bellevue lab

**Agent summary.** VERDICT ON THE CLAIM. "Maui lacks dental labs" is half-true and should be restated precisely: Maui has no independent commercial crown-and-bridge or implant laboratory that we could find, but it is not lab-free. Two tiny independent removable labs exist on paper: Pacific Rim Dental Laboratory (199 N Church St, Wailuku; David Eirinberg; dentures/partials/repairs only; opened 1978; live website, undated) and Elua II Dental Laboratory (1993 S Kihei Rd #214, Kihei; Clayton Mizufuka; SIC denture production; one 5.0 Google review; no website; 2025-26 activity unverified). A third directory listing, Precision Dental Technik LLC (33 Lono Ave #370, Kahului, Marco Kruger, "4 employees"), is almost certainly a ghost: the owner's LinkedIn dates his ownership Jan 2002 to Jan 2012 and lists him as a MicroDental consultant now, and Suite 370 at 33 Lono Ave is today Kahului Dental (a dentist office). More important for Integra: at least four Maui practices run captive digital labs (Maui Prosthodontics, Dr. Erik K. Wong, 5-axis mill, 3D printing, CEREC, in-house Ivotion dentures; Kai Dental, Kihei, Drs. Hollander and Roberts, on-site lab with full-time technician and 5-axis mill; Lahaina Smiles Dental, reopened at Lahaina Gateway with a "3D printing lab" and "crown milling center"; Island Smiles Dental, Kahului, markets an in-house lab), and at least seven practices advertise CEREC/same-day crowns (Kai Dental, Miyamoto Dental Design Suite, Dental Care of Maui, Smile Hawaii/Dr. Lance Ogata, Lahaina Smiles, Maui Prosthodontics, Smiles with Aloha per Healthgrades). With roughly 64 payroll dental offices on Maui (Census CBP 2023 via Groundwork) that is on the order of 10% of practices milling their own single units. Oahu, by contrast, has a dozen-plus independent labs (Honolulu Dental Laboratory, which says it has served "Oahu and the Neighbor Islands" for 60 years; Esthetic Dental Designs; Intuitive Dental Creations, full-arch; Premier; Kamaaina Prosthetic; Downtown Dental Lab; others), the Big Island has Kona Coast Dental Lab (est. 1977, accepts digital files, Seattle Study Club member) plus several small labs, and Kauai has two small labs. DENTIST COUNT (FACT). DCCA PVL licensing data as of Sept 30, 2024 (DBEDT Data Book Table 2.36) shows 116 dentists with a Maui-island address plus 3 Molokai and 1 Lanai (120 in Maui County), out of 1,140 in-state and 1,436 total Hawaii licensees (291 on the mainland). HIPHI's 2025 needs assessment counts 106 Maui dental providers in 2023 and a population-to-dentist ratio of 1,551:1 (statewide 1,320:1). Map listings show 76 dentist/orthodontist locations (Sept 2026). HRSA designates seven dental HPSAs in Maui County, but these are Medicaid/geographic access designations (Hana, Molokai, Lanai, community clinics), not evidence of unmet private restorative demand. HOW MAUI GETS LAB WORK TODAY (FACT/ESTIMATE). No Maui dentist page names its outside lab. The supply chain is inferable: Glidewell offers prepaid USPS Priority Mail labels at no cost (its $9 FedEx option is "contiguous U.S. only"), 3-day in-lab digital turnaround, and USPS's new Oct 1, 2026 standard is 2-4 days each way to Hawaii; Next Dental Lab (FL) explicitly markets Kahului with "about 5 business days of ground transit each way" and claims typical Hawaii lab fees of $105-200 for a zirconia crown (self-serving, unverified); Keating and PRO-Craft market Hawaii with 3-5 day delivery claims; Dandy is 100% digital with no Hawaii-specific statement found. Honolulu Dental Lab is the only Oahu lab that explicitly claims neighbor-island service; the shipping method is not described. MAUI ECONOMICS (FACT). Maui County adopted the 0.5% GET surcharge effective Jan 1, 2024 through Dec 31, 2030, so the retail GET rate on Maui is 4.5% (max pass-on 4.712%). Crucially, lab sales to dentists are NOT at that rate: HRS 237-24.3(6) excludes dental devices from the prosthetic-device exemption, but HRS 237-15 taxes technicians who supply dentists with prostheses "as though the technician were a manufacturer selling a product to a licensed retailer," i.e., the 0.5% wholesale rate, and the county surcharge does not apply to 0.5% activity (confirmed in the 2024 Form G-45 instructions). If Integra stays unregistered, the Maui dentist owes 0.5% use tax on landed value under HRS 238-2(2)(D). Act 41 (2018) economic nexus requires GET registration at $100,000 of Hawaii-delivered sales OR 200 transactions in the current or prior calendar year; for a lab, 200 cases/year (about 17/month) is the binding trigger. Foreign LLC registration is $50 (+$25 expedite, +$1 archives) with a $15 annual report; it is only needed if Integra physically transacts business in Hawaii. Hawaii does not license dental labs or technicians: HRS 448-3(a) expressly permits "any unlicensed person" to perform "mechanical work upon inert matter in a dental laboratory." BLS OEWS May 2025: Hawaii dental lab technicians, 120 employed, median $45,300, mean $47,560; no estimate is published for the Kahului-Wailuku MSA. Small Maui office space lists at $1.35-$1.65/SF/mo NNN (691 SF sublease, Kihei) to $2.50-$4.00/SF/mo NNN (Kahului/Wailuku), plus CAM ($0.48-$0.94/SF) and GET passed through at 4.712%. Travel: SEA-OGG nonstop daily (Alaska 26/wk, Delta 7/wk); HVCB quarterly average round-trip base fare from Seattle $602-$697 (Q2 2025-Q1 2026); Google Flights typical $365-780; midsize rental car about $82/day shoulder season ($312/wk Aug 2026, $635/wk June 2026); Maui hotel ADR $526 and vacation-rental ADR $649 in Aug 2026, with Wailea/Kihei rentals about $438-$453. OBTAINABLE DEMAND (ESTIMATE). Maui is a small, well-served island: ~64 practices, ~10% self-milling, nationally marketed labs with free USPS inbound and 3-5 day in-lab times, an Oahu lab tier, and $129 Integra zirconia priced above Glidewell-tier and far above Next's $39. Integra has no Hawaii-specific advantage except relationships (the Sept-Oct 2026 trip, Seattle Study Club ties via the Maui Professional Dental Forum chapter) and Alaska Air Cargo potential that is not yet operational. A defensible base case is 2-4 Maui practices sending recurring work within 12 months, 15-40 units/month, with upside of 6-8 practices and 50-80 units/month only if Integra works the island in person. The highest-value Maui angles are not single crowns: full-arch cases generated by Maui Jaw Surgical Institute and Maui Oral Surgery (both "same-day teeth"/All-on-4 centers whose GPs need finals), ti-bar/superstructure work for Kai Dental and Miyamoto, and ARUM/MillBox sales and service to the practices already milling in-house (Lahaina Smiles, Kai, Maui Prosthodontics, Island Smiles), where Maui has zero local service capability. Switch triggers that matter: digital-only Rx with no inbound shipping, a published Hawaii delivery commitment (e.g., scan-to-door 6 business days), transparent pricing at or below mainland tiers, full-arch/ti-bar depth, and periodic on-island visits. Nothing found supports calling Maui "underserved" for restorative lab work.

**Findings.**

- *fact, medium confidence.* Maui has no independent commercial crown-and-bridge/implant lab found; it has two tiny independent denture labs and several captive in-office digital labs. Value: 0 independent C&B labs; 2 independent removable labs; 4+ captive labs. [source](https://exa.ai/library/places?q=5f63a20d948fcfcb) (2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Pacific Rim Dental Laboratory, Wailuku, is a one-technician denture/partial lab, not a crown-and-bridge lab. Value: 1 employee (directory estimate). [source](https://pacificrimdentallab.com/) (undated)
- *unknown, medium confidence.* Elua II Dental Laboratory, Kihei, exists in directories as a denture-production lab; current activity unverified. Value: status: directory-listed, activity unverified. [source](https://hawaii.health-serve.org/284402-elua_ii_dental_laboratory.htm) (undated)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Precision Dental Technik LLC (Kahului) appears defunct; directory listings are stale. Value: closed circa 2012 (inferred). [source](https://linkedin.com/in/marco-kruger-242a2a26) (2026-09-30)
- *fact, high confidence.* Maui Prosthodontics claims to be the only dental office and laboratory in Hawaii fabricating Ivotion digital dentures fully in-house, with 5-axis milling and CEREC. [source](https://www.mauiprosth.com/services) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* Kai Dental (Kihei) operates an on-site lab with a full-time technician and a 5-axis mill and does All-on-4 with zirconia finals. [source](https://kaidentalmaui.com/technology/) (undated)
- *fact, medium confidence.* At least seven Maui practices advertise CEREC or same-day in-office crowns, roughly 10% of Maui practices. Value: 7 of ~64-76 practices. [source](https://www.drogata.com/Blog/Post/Embrace-Cutting-Edge-Dentistry-Transform-Your-Smile-With-CEREC-Technology) (2025-06-18)
- *fact, high confidence.* Oahu has a dozen-plus independent dental labs; Honolulu Dental Laboratory explicitly claims neighbor-island service. Value: 16+ Oahu labs identified. [source](https://honoluludentallab.wixsite.com/smile) (undated)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Big Island and Kauai each have small independent labs. [source](https://konacoastdentallab.com/about-us/) (2022-07-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* 116 dentists hold Maui-island addresses (120 in Maui County) out of 1,140 in-state Hawaii licensees. Value: 116 (Maui island); 120 (Maui County). [source](https://files.hawaii.gov/dbedt/economic/databook/2024-individual/02/023624.pdf) (2024-09-30)
- *fact, high confidence.* HIPHI counts 106 Maui dental providers in 2023 and a 1,551:1 population-to-dentist ratio; only 29.2% accept Medicaid. Value: 106 providers; 1,551:1. [source](https://www.hiphi.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/2025-Community-Oral-Health-Needs-Assessment-Environmental-Scan.pdf) (2025-09-06)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Maui County has ~64 dental offices with payroll (2023) and 76 dentist/orthodontist map listings (Sept 2026). Value: 64 payroll offices; 76 listings. [source](https://www.groundworkdemographics.com/markets/dentists/hawaii/maui-county) (2026-09)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Hawaii Dental Association has 997 members in four county societies including the Maui County Dental Society; MCDS has no website found. Value: 997 HDA members statewide. [source](https://www.hawaiidentalassociation.net/about) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* Glidewell's $9 FedEx option is contiguous-U.S. only; Hawaii dentists use free prepaid USPS Priority Mail labels, 2-4 business days each way. Value: $0 USPS; 2-4 days each way. [source](https://glidewelldental.com/send-case/pickup-and-delivery) (2026-05-05)
- *fact, high confidence.* USPS lengthened Hawaii service expectations effective Oct 1, 2026: Priority Mail 2-4 days, Ground Advantage 10+ days. Value: Priority Mail 2-4 days; Ground 10+ days. [source](https://www.supplychaindive.com/news/usps-lengthens-some-delivery-expectations-outside-contiguous-us/831958/) (2026-10-05)
- *estimate, low confidence.* Next Dental Lab (Florida) explicitly markets Kahului/Maui with ~5 business days ground transit each way and claims Hawaii lab fees of $105-200 for a zirconia crown. Value: zirconia $39 vs claimed HI $105-200. [source](https://signup.nextdentallab.com/dental-lab/hawaii) (undated)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Keating (Irvine CA) and PRO-Craft market Hawaii; PRO-Craft claims 3-5 business day delivery and a free iTero program. [source](https://www.pro-craft.com/dental-laboratory-kailua-hawaii) (undated)
- *unknown, medium confidence.* Dandy is 100% digital (Lehi UT, Carrollton TX labs); no Hawaii-specific shipping or turnaround statement found; NDX Hawaii policy not found. [source](https://www.meetdandy.com/faster-partial-dentures/) (2026-03-19)
- *fact, high confidence.* Maui County adopted the 0.5% GET county surcharge effective Jan 1, 2024 through Dec 31, 2030; Maui retail GET is 4.5%, maximum pass-on 4.712%. Value: 4.5% (4.712% pass-on). [source](https://tax.hawaii.gov/geninfo/countysurcharge/) (2024-01-09)
- *fact, high confidence.* Dental prosthetics are NOT exempt from GET: HRS 237-24.3(6)'s prosthetic-device exemption expressly excludes dental devices. [source](https://www.capitol.hawaii.gov/hrscurrent/Vol04_Ch0201-0257/HRS0237/HRS_0237-0024_0003.htm) (undated (current HRS))
- *fact, high confidence.* Lab sales of prostheses to dentists are taxed at the 0.5% wholesale GET rate under HRS 237-15, not 4.5%, and the county surcharge does not apply. Value: 0.5% of gross (about $0.65 on a $129 crown). [source](https://www.capitol.hawaii.gov/hrscurrent/Vol04_Ch0201-0257/HRS0237/HRS_0237-0015.htm) (undated (current HRS))
- *fact, high confidence.* If the mainland lab is unregistered, the Hawaii dentist owes 0.5% use tax on the landed value of imported lab work. Value: 0.5% of landed value. [source](https://www.capitol.hawaii.gov/hrscurrent/Vol04_Ch0201-0257/HRS0238/HRS_0238-0002.htm) (undated (current HRS))
- *fact, high confidence.* Hawaii economic nexus (Act 41, 2018) requires GET registration at $100,000 of Hawaii-delivered gross income OR 200 separate transactions in the current or preceding calendar year. Value: $100,000 or 200 transactions. [source](https://files.hawaii.gov/tax/news/announce/ann18-10%5Famended.pdf) (2018-07)
- *fact, high confidence.* Hawaii foreign LLC registration costs $50 (+$25 expedited, +$1 State Archives fee); LLC annual report $15. Value: $50 one-time; $15/yr. [source](https://cca.hawaii.gov/breg/registration/dgp/fllc-fees/) (2026-01-22)
- *fact, high confidence.* Hawaii does not license dental laboratories or dental technicians. [source](https://files.hawaii.gov/dcca/pvl/pvl/hrs/hrs_pvl_448.pdf) (undated (current HRS))
- *fact, medium confidence.* Hawaii dental laboratory technician wages: 120 employed statewide, median $45,300, mean $47,560 (May 2025); no Kahului-Wailuku MSA estimate published. Value: median $45,300/yr (~$21.78/hr). [source](https://wagedex.com/jobs/dental-laboratory-technicians) (2026 (May 2025 OEWS release))
- *fact, high confidence.* Small Maui office/flex space currently lists at $1.35-$4.00/SF/month NNN plus CAM and GET pass-through. Value: $1.35-$4.00/SF/mo NNN. [source](https://www.commercialsearch.com/commercial-property/us/hi/kihei/professional-office-spaces-available/) (2026-09-19)

### B.6 Alaska market

*Scope:* Alaska (Anchorage, Mat-Su, Fairbanks, Juneau) as an overlooked dental-lab market for Integra Dental Design (Bellevue, WA)

**Agent summary.** Bottom line: Alaska is small, high-fee, logistically reachable same-day from Seattle, and already served by a handful of credible Anchorage labs plus several in-house labs. It is not "underserved" on the evidence; it is thin outside Anchorage and thin on independent digital/implant milling capacity. Obtainable demand for Integra is modest and skewed toward (a) lab-to-lab milling for small Anchorage labs, (b) digital-only GP offices in Fairbanks, Juneau and Mat-Su that have no local crown-and-bridge lab, and (c) implant-restoring GPs working with the oral-surgeon referral network who lack Take 2's or Woodside's in-house technician. FACTS (sourced). Dentist supply: the Alaska Board of Dental Examiners had 798 active dentist licenses on Jan 31, 2026 (Legislative Audit), with only 2 prosthodontist, 7 periodontist and 23 oral-surgeon specialty licenses; ADA HPI's 2025 workforce report puts Alaska at 78.4 dentists per 100,000 in 2024 vs 59.5 nationally, which on the state's 738,737 population implies ~580 professionally active dentists (estimate). Census CBP (2023) counts 315 dental offices in Alaska. NPI-based counts put ~472 dental providers in Anchorage, 151 in Fairbanks, 100 in Wasilla. Fees: Alaska's measured average porcelain crown is $1,644 (+18% vs $1,399 US; ASQ360/CareCredit 2023-24), HPI-based private estimate $1,414 (+8%), and Alaska Medicaid pays $827 for D2740, the highest listed; Alaska ranks 4th on a restorative cost index. Local labs physically in Alaska with websites: Castable Ceramics (Anchorage, since 1986, now an Apex Dental Laboratory Group lab; FCZ, e.max, PFZ, custom Ti/Zr abutments, local pickup); Precision Dental Designs (Anchorage; Prettau zirconia, hybrid bridges, Pekkton, removables; says it also serves the lower 48 via free FedEx labels); Trinity Dental Lab (Anchorage; Jin S. Seo CDT, 30 yrs; zirconia, e.max, All-on-X, same-day repairs); Evo Dental Milling and Design (Anchorage, same address as Take 2 Dental Implant Studio; published prices: FCZ on Ti-base $399, custom Ti abutment $149, cement-retained FCZ $129, zirconia full arch $2,999-$3,499, Ti bar $999, printed full-arch temp $399). Directory-only listings (unverified): Gnathestics Dental Lab and Brown's Dental Plastics (Anchorage), Eagle Dental Lab (Eagle River, owner on LinkedIn since 2003), Wasilla Dental Lab, Valley Dental Lab and Juneau Ceramic Studio (Juneau), Peninsula Dental Laboratory (Sterling). In-house production: Take 2 Dental Implant Studio (full-arch only, zirconia/Ti milled in-house, "no outsourcing"), Woodside Dental Group (in-house technician; $22,999/arch, $45,999 full mouth all-in zirconia), Anchorage Midtown Dental (on-site lab for one-day crowns), and at least ten CEREC/same-day-crown practices across Anchorage, Mat-Su, Fairbanks and Juneau. Southcentral Foundation (tribal) runs its own prosthodontic clinic and labs and in Aug 2026 was hiring six dental lab technicians ($30.14-$40.18/hr) for three clinics; its contracted-dentist RFPs route implant and full-mouth work to SCF Prosthodontics, so tribal programs are not a near-term lab customer. DSO penetration is low (~4.8% of providers; MB2 Dental 10 providers; Pacific Dental Alliance in Fairbanks; btyDENTAL 8 Anchorage offices). National labs reach Alaska with friction: Glidewell's $9 FedEx overnight is contiguous-US only (Alaska gets free USPS Priority at 2-4 days each way); Next Dental Lab (FL) markets to Alaska with ~5 business days ground each way; VTA excludes AK/HI from its 7-day promise; Dandy is digital-only (UT/TX labs) with no Alaska-specific policy found. Logistics: Alaska Air Cargo GoldStreak, Seattle (Zone 4) to Anchorage (Zone 2), is $74 minimum for up to 1 lb plus $2.56/lb (letter $49) effective Oct 1, 2026 (up from $69/$44 in Jan 2026), plus a $0.05/lb security fee ($12.50 min); tender 60 min before departure, pick up 60 min after arrival; SEA cargo (2600 S 165th St) and ANC cargo (4100 Old International Airport Rd) accept GoldStreak 24/7; Alaska flies ~107 SEA-ANC nonstops weekly. Known Shipper status is required only for pieces of 16 oz or more, so an Alaska dentist can tender a single-crown case to Integra without being vetted. FedEx One Rate (Alaska included): 2Day small box $19.55, Standard Overnight small box $101.75, Priority Overnight small box $148.20; FedEx Priority Overnight has no Saturday delivery to Alaska. UPS Next Day Air 2026 daily list to Alaska zone 124: 1 lb $127.35, 2 lb $139.78. Taxes/regulatory: no state sales tax; Anchorage levies none (two 3% proposals were postponed indefinitely, Mar 2025 and Jan 2026); Mat-Su Borough 0% but Palmer 3% and Wasilla levies a city tax; Juneau has a sales tax. Foreign LLC registration $350, biennial report $200, business license $50/yr. Alaska does not register dental labs or technicians; lab work is lawful on a dentist's prescription (AS 08.36.360(4)); only about a dozen states (FL, KY, TX, SC, etc.) regulate labs. Reach: Alaska Dental Society (1407 W 31st Ave, Anchorage, 907-563-3003) held its 2026 annual meeting May 22-23 in Seward (implant-focused CE) and will hold 2027's on Feb 18-19 at Waikoloa, Hawaii, with a vendor marketplace whose sponsorships "sell out" (commitments due Dec 2026). Components: Anchorage DS (irregular), Mat-Su DS (irregular), North Central DS in Fairbanks (2nd Tuesday monthly), Southeast DS (1st Wednesday), Juneau (irregular). No Alaska Seattle Study Club affiliate or implant study club was found. ESTIMATES. Lab density: ~8-12 identifiable commercial labs for 580-800 dentists (1 per 50-100) vs Washington's ~175 lab establishments for ~5,000 dentists (1 per ~29). Obtainable demand: 5-10 accounts and 30-80 units/month within 12-18 months is a judgment, not a measured figure. Switching barriers are real: 30-40-year local lab relationships with pickup, high CEREC penetration, in-house full-arch at the two most aggressive implant practices, weather-exposed air logistics, and a dentist population one-ninth of Washington's. What favors Integra: same-day air transit that beats Anchorage labs for Fairbanks/Juneau on time, wholesale pricing well under Evo's published retail (e.g., $22 mill-only vs $129-$399 FCZ; $650 vs $2,999 zirconia arch) for the small Anchorage labs, the owner's existing Known Shipper status, and Seattle being the natural CE and travel hub. UNKNOWNS. No Census count of Alaska lab establishments (API key required); no FedEx standard-list "To Alaska" table retrieved; no public statement found of an Alaska dentist naming a Seattle lab; ADS exhibitor fees; actual negotiated courier discounts; Alaska dentists' current turnaround expectations (only inferences from CEREC adoption and national-lab transit claims).

**Findings.**

- *fact, high confidence.* Alaska had 798 active dentist licenses as of January 31, 2026; specialty licenses included 23 oral & maxillofacial surgery, 7 periodontics, 14 endodontics, 2 prosthodontics; 3,179 total dental licenses/certificates/permits.. Value: 798 dentist licenses; 2 prosthodontists; 23 OMS. [source](https://legaudit.akleg.gov/wp-content/uploads/20150_Final_BDE_Web.pdf) (data as of 2026-01-31 (report undated))
- *fact, medium confidence.* ADA HPI puts Alaska at about 78.4 dentists per 100,000 population in 2024 (US 59.5), among the highest state ratios; Alaska's ratio fell 1.8% from 2014 to 2024.. Value: 78.4 per 100K (2024); US 59.5. [source](https://www.ada.org/-/media/project/ada-organization/ada/ada-org/files/resources/research/hpi/US_dentist_workforce_2025.pdf) (2025 (data as of August 2025))
- *estimate, medium confidence.* Implied professionally active dentists in Alaska in 2024-25: roughly 580.. Value: ~580 dentists. [source](https://live.laborstats.alaska.gov/data-pages/alaska-population-estimates) (2025 estimates released 2026-01-28)
- *fact, high confidence.* Alaska population July 1, 2025: 738,737 statewide; Anchorage 289,221; Mat-Su Borough 117,412; Fairbanks North Star Borough 97,083; Juneau 31,182.. Value: 738,737 / 289,221 / 117,412 / 97,083 / 31,182. [source](https://live.laborstats.alaska.gov/data-pages/alaska-population-estimates) (2026-01-28)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Alaska has 315 dental office establishments (NAICS 621210) employing 2,417 (Census County Business Patterns 2023).. Value: 315 offices; 2,417 employees. [source](https://plainbizbench.com/state/AK/) (2023 data, page undated)
- *fact, medium confidence.* NPI-registered dental providers by Alaska metro: Anchorage 472, Fairbanks 151, Wasilla 100; 1,285 statewide (includes hygienists); DSO-affiliated share ~4.8% (62 providers).. Value: 472 / 151 / 100; DSO 4.8%. [source](https://providersignal.com/territory/ak) (undated (2026))
- *fact, medium confidence.* Alaska dental fees are among the highest in the US: measured average porcelain crown $1,644 (+18% vs $1,399 national), 46th of 51 markets; restorative cost index 130 (4th highest) with implant $5,450 and denture $2,371.. Value: Crown $1,644; implant $5,450. [source](https://realdentalcosts.com/en/dental-costs/cost-comparison-by-state/) (2023-2024 study; page 2026)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Alaska Medicaid's fee-for-service rate for D2740 (porcelain/ceramic crown) is $827, the highest state Medicaid crown fee in the compiled list; Medicaid paid an average $386 per claim line.. Value: $827 fee schedule; $386 paid/line. [source](https://realdentalcosts.com/en/dental-codes/d2740/) (2023-2024 data)
- *fact, high confidence.* Castable Ceramics (Anchorage) is a full-service lab operating since 1986 and is now part of the Apex Dental Laboratory Group (21 labs, Waco TX); it offers full-contour zirconia, e.max, PFZ, PFM, cast metal, custom titanium/zirconia abutments, removables and local pickup.. Value: Since 1986; Apex network. [source](https://www.apexlabgroup.com/castable-ceramics) (undated)

Seo, CDT (30 years), offering zirconia, e.max, PFM, full cast, custom abutments, screw/cement-retained implant crowns, All-on-X, dentures, and same/next-day repairs ('call before 10 am ... ready by 4:30 pm').. - *fact, high confidence.* Alaska Air Cargo GoldStreak (next-flight, no prebooking) from Seattle (Zone 4) to Anchorage (Zone 2): $74 minimum charge covering the first pound, $2.56/lb thereafter ($2.34/lb at 30 lb); GoldStreak Letter (<1 lb in Alaska letter packaging) $49. Anchorage to Seattle: $72 minimum, letter $47. Seattle to Juneau (Zone 3): $74 minimum, letter $49. Rates effective October 1, 2026.. Value: SEA->ANC $74 min / $49 letter. [source](https://public.alaskacargo.com/getmedia/33034730-1e1b-4764-8440-f85f4e679c15/GoldStreakShippingRates_OCT2026.pdf) (effective 2026-10-01)
- *fact, high confidence.* GoldStreak operating rules: tender at the cargo facility 60 minutes before departure; available for pickup 60 minutes after arrival; guarantee (up to 50% refund) only if booked in advance; domestic security screening fee $0.05/lb with $12.50 minimum (CCSF shippers exempt); dangerous-goods fee $95.. Value: 60 min before / 60 min after; $12.50 min screening. [source](https://www.alaskacargo.com/services/rates) (undated)

- *fact, high confidence.* The State of Alaska levies no sales tax; the Municipality of Anchorage levies no general sales tax (0%), nor does the Matanuska-Susitna Borough; Palmer levies 3%, North Pole 5.5%; Juneau and Wasilla levy city sales taxes.. Value: Anchorage 0%; Palmer 3%; Juneau 5% (commonly cited). - *fact, high confidence.* Alaska foreign (non-Alaskan) LLC registration costs $350 (form 08-497); biennial report $200 ($247.50 if late), due January 2 every other year; Alaska business license $50 per year; a foreign entity may not transact business in Alaska until registered.. Value: $350 + $200/2 yrs + $50/yr. [source](https://www.commerce.alaska.gov/web/cbpl/Corporations/CorpFormsFees.aspx) (undated)

- *estimate, medium confidence.* Turnaround expectations in Alaska are not documented publicly; inference: with national labs quoting 2-5 business days of transit each way to Alaska and 12+ CEREC practices, Alaska GPs currently either mill in-office, use local Anchorage labs with pickup, or accept roughly two-week national-lab cycles. GoldStreak same-day plus a 2-day Integra cycle could deliver 3-4 business days door-to-door, faster than national labs and competitive with Anchorage labs for Fairbanks/Juneau offices.. Value: 5-10 accounts; 30-80 units/mo. [source](https://evodentalmilling.com/) (compiled 2026-10-12)

- Whether Alaska Air Cargo screening fees apply to GoldStreak letters under 1 lb and whether Integra's Known Shipper record requires a credit account for invoicing.

### B.7 Dallas-Fort Worth and Miami markets

*Scope:* Dallas-Fort Worth (TX) and Miami / South Florida as candidate remote-served markets for Integra Dental Design (Bellevue, WA): dentist and lab supply, full-arch/DSO demand structure, state lab regulation, tax and entity costs, labor, real estate, flights and parcel logistics; conclusion on obtainable accounts in 12 months

**Agent summary.** Bottom line: neither Dallas-Fort Worth nor South Florida gives a Bellevue lab a structural edge over local labs or national mail-order labs. Of the two, DFW is the less-bad option, and only as a lab-to-lab milling and equipment-dealer play; South Florida has no structural case and should be deprioritized behind Southern California, where Integra already has accounts and family presence. FACTS. (1) Regulation is asymmetric. Texas Occupations Code 266.151 requires a registration certificate for any person who "provide[s] or offer[s] to provide dental laboratory services," and 266.153 covers "each dental laboratory doing business in this state"; nothing in ch. 266, 22 TAC ch. 116, or TSBDE's "Do I Need a Dental Laboratory Registration" chart exempts an out-of-state lab, and 266.102(b)(5)(D)-(E) gives the Board rulemaking power over prescriptions referred to out-of-state labs. Registration is $125 initial / $136 annual (TSBDE 2022 application form; current schedule page would not render) but requires a Certified Dental Technician "employed by and working on the premises" at least 30 hours/week (22 TAC 116.5) and a jurisprudence assessment. The brief does not say whether Integra employs a CDT; if it does not, Texas is a compliance problem before it is a sales problem. Florida is the opposite: Fla. Stat. 466.032(5)(d)2 states an out-of-state lab serving a Florida dentist "is not required to register"; voluntary registration costs $200 (Board page) with 18 CE hours biennially. Both states exempt dental prostheses from sales tax (Tex. Tax Code 151.313(a)(5) "dental, or prosthetic device"; Fla. Stat. 212.08(2)(a) "dentures... prosthetic and orthopedic appliances," Rule 12A-1.021). Foreign-LLC registration is $750 in Texas (BOC 4.152(3) via 4.154) plus an annual franchise report (no tax below the $2.65M 2026-27 threshold) and $125 plus $138.75/yr in Florida. (2) Lab supply is the decisive asymmetry. Census CBP 2023 (via two secondary tabulations) shows Florida with 424 dental-lab establishments, second in the nation, versus Texas 277 and Washington 175. BLS OEWS May 2023 shows the Miami metro with 800 dental lab technicians at a location quotient of 1.31 versus DFW 670 at 0.75 (Texas statewide LQ 0.57, Florida 1.12). South Florida is over-supplied with lab capacity relative to the U.S.; DFW is under-supplied and already imports lab work. A directory count (poidata, June 2026) lists 97 labs in Miami and 42 in Hialeah alone. South Florida labs advertise 48-hour zirconia with free pickup across Miami-Dade and Broward (Rivfor), 24-hour rush (Esthetic Dental Laboratory), 3-day standard (Vita), and same-day printed immediate-load arches for Broward/Palm Beach surgeons (Dental Lab Miami). DFW labs are fewer and advertise slower service (American Dental Laboratory 10 days in lab; Luna Labs 5-7 business days). (3) Dentist counts are comparable. Texas DSHS (Sept 2020): Dallas 1,678, Tarrant 1,093, Collin 884, Denton 450 (4,105). Florida DOH 2019-20 survey: Miami-Dade 1,783, Broward 1,476, Palm Beach 1,110 (4,369, with some dual-county reporting). Newer official counts exist (DSHS 2024 dashboard; FL 2023-24 report) but were not machine-readable. (4) Demand structure works against a remote full-arch lab. Most full-arch centers in both metros run in-house labs (DFW: Advanced Dentistry, ARCHPOINT, Texas Implant & Dental Center, Forte, The Dental Implant Place at 98% in-house, Innate, Dallas All-on-4 Center; South Florida: Nuvia Miami, OneSolution, Done In One, In A Day Smile, Smiland, Boca Dental, Art Dental Studio). They are overflow or titanium-bar targets at best. DFW is a DSO headquarters hub (MB2 850+ practices, Jefferson 60+, DECA/Ideal, MINT with a "MINT-owned dental lab," Community Dental Partners 70+, Apex Dental Partners 50+, Rodeo 50+), but MB2 runs lab spend through a Procurement Lab Manager who owns a "preferred lab strategy," and Leixir maintains an MB2 landing page and a Carrollton, TX center. A $140k/yr lab will not displace that in 12 months. South Florida's largest DSO is Sage Dental (Boca Raton, 150+ offices); its lab sourcing is unknown. (5) Logistics and price. SEA-DFW: about 100 nonstops a week (American, Alaska, Delta), 4 hours, Kayak 12-month average round trip $392. SEA-MIA: 21-25 nonstops a week (one daily each on American, Alaska, Delta), about 6 hours, average round trip $438, 3-hour time difference. UPS 2026 list rates: 2nd Day Air 1 lb $45.75 (zone 7, Dallas) / $46.67 (zone 8, Miami); Ground $14.83 / $15.03 but 4-5 business days (zones are my estimate from mileage; fuel surcharge and account discounts not included). National labs already ship into both metros at $89-$122 for monolithic zirconia in 3 days (Crown World $89 digital, ROE zRc $93), so Integra's $129 dentist price is not an edge. Its lab-to-lab prices ($22 mill-only crown, $650 full-arch from STL, $299 ti-bar) are well below Mabel ($39-59 crown; $999 copy-mill to $1,999 full package) and ROE ($2,420 final arch), so the only credible wedge is selling idle 5-axis capacity to other labs, with $15-47 freight per case eroding it. ESTIMATES. DFW: 2-5 obtainable accounts in 12 months, realistically 1-2 lab-to-lab milling customers among small DFW labs lacking wet 5-axis mills, plus 1-3 dentist accounts from personal or ARUM-dealer relationships; incremental revenue perhaps $1-4k/month, contingent on resolving TSBDE registration and the CDT requirement. The stronger DFW angle is the ARUM dealership (no territory restriction) into a lab-short metro, which can create captive milling and training customers. South Florida: 0-2 accounts and no structural reason to win: lab-dense, price-aggressive (a Hialeah lab itself cites $35-80 offshore crowns), same-day local service, thinnest flight schedule, and no existing relationships. UNKNOWNS that matter: county-level CBP lab counts; whether TSBDE actually enforces registration on out-of-state labs; whether Integra has a CDT; latest county dentist counts; Sage Dental's lab sourcing; negotiated parcel discounts.

**Findings.**

- *fact, high confidence.* Texas requires a registration certificate to provide or offer dental laboratory services; statute applies to each lab 'doing business in this state' with no express out-of-state exemption. Value: registration required; no geographic carve-out. [source](https://statutes.capitol.texas.gov/Docs/OC/htm/OC.266.htm) (undated (statute current through 2017 amendments))
- *fact, high confidence.* Texas-registered labs must employ a Certified Dental Technician on premises at least 30 hours/week. Value: CDT on premises >=30 hrs/wk. [source](https://www.law.cornell.edu/regulations/texas/22-Tex-Admin-Code-SS-116-5) (undated)
- *historical, medium confidence.* TSBDE dental lab registration fee $125 initial; $136 annual renewal; renew by Dec 31; jurisprudence assessment every 3 years. Value: $125 initial; $136/yr renewal. [source](https://tsbde.texas.gov/78i8ljhbj/Initial-Dental-Lab_2022.pdf) (2022 (form date))
- *fact, high confidence.* TSBDE's 'Do I Need a Dental Laboratory Registration in Texas' decision chart contains no exemption based on the lab's location. Value: no out-of-state exemption. [source](https://tsbde.texas.gov/78i8ljhbj/2019/03/Do-I-Need-A-Dental-Laboratory-Registration-in-Texas.pdf) (2019-03 (file path))
- *fact, high confidence.* Texas CE rule: exempt labs' designated employee needs 12 CE hours/yr; otherwise proof the designated CDT maintains certification. Value: 12 CE hrs/yr (exempt labs) or current CDT. [source](https://www.law.cornell.edu/regulations/texas/22-Tex-Admin-Code-SS-116-6) (undated (amended eff. 2009-02-26))
- *fact, high confidence.* Texas-registered labs must label or certify whether a prosthesis was made entirely by them, by another domestic lab, or by a foreign lab (with country). Value: origin disclosure required. [source](https://www.law.cornell.edu/regulations/texas/22-Tex-Admin-Code-SS-116-10) (undated (amended eff. 2010-12-22))
- *fact, high confidence.* Florida: out-of-state labs serving Florida dentists are not required to register. Value: exempt; in-state fee cap $300 biennial; 18 CE hrs. [source](https://www.leg.state.fl.us/statutes/index.cfm?App_mode=Display_Statute&URL=0400-0499/0466/Sections/0466.032.html) (undated (history through ch. 2016-230))
- *fact, high confidence.* Florida Board of Dentistry dental laboratory registration application fee is $200 (non-refundable); rules in F.A.C. chapter 64B27. Value: $200 application fee (voluntary for out-of-state). [source](https://floridasdentistry.gov/dental-laboratory/) (2025-04-29 (page); 2021-06-13 (rule))
- *fact, high confidence.* Texas exempts dental and prosthetic devices from sales and use tax. Value: exempt. [source](https://texas.public.law/statutes/tex._tax_code_section_151.313) (undated (last amended eff. 2023-09-01))
- *fact, high confidence.* Florida exempts dentures and prescription prosthetic appliances from sales tax. Value: exempt. [source](https://www.leg.state.fl.us/statutes/index.cfm?App_mode=Display_Statute&URL=0200-0299/0212/Sections/0212.08.html) (undated (statute); rule eff. 2010-07-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Texas foreign LLC registration fee is $750. Value: $750 one-time. [source](https://texas.public.law/statutes/tex._bus._orgs._code_section_4.152) (undated (verified by mirror 2025-05-26))
- *fact, high confidence.* Texas franchise tax: no tax due below $2,650,000 total revenue for 2026-2027 reports, but an annual Public Information Report is still required; $50 late-filing penalty. **Unknowns the agent could not resolve.**

- County-level Census CBP counts of NAICS 339116 dental laboratories for Dallas, Tarrant, Collin, Denton, Miami-Dade, Broward and Palm Beach (Census API requires a key; data.census.gov tables are JavaScript-rendered). Only state-level 2023 figures (FL 424, TX 277, WA 175) and directory counts were obtainable.
- Whether TSBDE in practice requires or enforces registration for out-of-state laboratories that ship to Texas dentists; the statute and TSBDE chart contain no exemption, but no enforcement record or Board FAQ on out-of-state labs was found.
- Whether any Integra employee holds CDT certification (required on-premises 30 hrs/week under 22 TAC 116.5 if Integra registers in Texas).
- Current (FY2026) TSBDE dental laboratory fees; the fee-schedule page is JavaScript-only and did not render, so 2022 application figures ($125/$136) are used.
- Most recent official county dentist counts: Texas DSHS 2022-2024 county supply tables are on a Tableau dashboard that could not be read; the Florida DOH 2023-2024 dentist workforce PDF (uploaded Feb 2026) did not parse. ADA HPI county-level counts were not retrieved.
- Sage Dental's (and Dental Care Alliance's) laboratory sourcing model and whether any South Florida DSO operates a captive lab.
- Terms and incumbents of DSO lab contracts in DFW (MB2's preferred labs beyond the Leixir landing page; Jefferson, DECA/Ideal, CDP, Apex Dental Partners, Rodeo sourcing).
- ClearChoice DFW and South Florida centers' lab arrangement (ClearChoice Fort Lauderdale page confirmed; in-center lab status not verified in this research).
- Exact UPS/FedEx zones from ZIP 98007 to Dallas/Miami ZIPs, applicable fuel surcharge, and Integra's negotiated discount; FedEx list rates could not be fetched (fedex.com blocks crawlers).
- Whether milling/lab capacity actually constrains any specific DFW lab (the lab-to-lab thesis assumes some small DFW labs lack wet 5-axis mills; no lab publicly states this).
- Spanish-language service expectations and referral networks in Miami-Dade dentistry (not researched; could matter for a remote English-only lab).
- Whether shipping finished prostheses into TX/FL without staff or property triggers foreign-qualification or franchise-tax nexus (statutory interstate-commerce exclusions not fetched; verify with counsel).

### B.8 Logistics

*Scope:* Carrier logistics from Bellevue, WA 98007 to candidate markets (SoCal, Maui/Honolulu, Anchorage, Dallas, Miami, Eugene, Spokane, Boise): transit commitments, 2026 published rates, surcharges, drop-off/return-leg logistics, Alaska Air Cargo GoldStreak, discounts, claims, disruption risk

**Agent summary.** Facts first. (1) "Overnight" to Hawaii is not overnight for most of the lab's realistic options. FedEx's 2026 list-rate tables carry separate Alaska/Hawaii zones (9/10 metro, 11/12 rural) and footnotes: Express Saver is not offered to AK/HI at all; Standard Overnight is "Alaska metro only"; 2Day A.M. is "Alaska only"; Priority Overnight and 2Day are the Hawaii-inbound workhorses, and the FedEx service guide language is "delivery in 2 business days for certain shipments to and from Alaska and Hawaii" (Priority Overnight) and "3 business days for certain shipments" (2Day). UPS's 2026 zone files confirm the mirror image: from the 48 states, only Next Day Air, 2nd Day Air and Ground are offered to AK/HI (zones 124/224/44 metro; 126/226/46 extended); Next Day Air Saver, 2nd Day Air A.M. and 3 Day Select are not. Kahului 96732, Kihei 96753 and Lahaina 96761 fall in UPS's extended tier; Honolulu 968xx and Anchorage 995xx are metro. UPS's own guide says Next Day Air to Hawaii "may require additional time in transit"; Next Day Air Early is explicitly committed to Anchorage by 8:30/9:00 a.m. Saturday delivery in Hawaii (FedEx) is Oahu-only, select ZIPs. (2) Published 2026 list prices for a 1 lb crown box, with the fuel surcharge in force the week of Oct 5, 2026 (FedEx Express 31.75%, Ground 29.25%; UPS Air 32.25%, Ground 29.75%): Bellevue to Maui, UPS Next Day Air ~$169 (list) or ~$101 under UPS's published Small Business Rates; UPS 2nd Day Air ~$108 list / ~$71 SBR; FedEx Priority Overnight ~$172-177; FedEx 2Day ~$90 (metro) to ~$115 (rural). Bellevue to Anchorage is priced identically to Hawaii metro at both carriers (FedEx PO ~$172, UPS NDA ~$168, UPS 2DA ~$89). Within the contiguous states the same box runs FedEx Priority Overnight ~$88 (zone 3, Eugene/Spokane est.) to ~$150 (zone 8, Miami); UPS 2nd Day Air ~$38-62; Ground ~$16-20. The cheapest credible expedited options to the islands are flat-rate products that bundle fuel: FedEx One Rate 2Day small box is $19.55 nationwide including AK/HI (FedEx packaging only; 2Day to HI/AK can be 3 business days and carries no money-back guarantee); UPS Simple Rate 2nd Day Air extra-small box is $48.80 to metro AK/HI and $90.05 to remote AK/HI, no fuel surcharge. (3) Alaska Air Cargo GoldStreak is the only true same-day product. Oct 1, 2026 tariff: SEA (zone 4) to OGG/HNL (zone 11) letter $59, minimum $99 (1-3 lb all pay the minimum at $3.44/lb); SEA to ANC (zone 2) letter $49, minimum $74; SEA to Orange County (SNA, zone 6) letter $49, minimum $69; DFW/MIA $79. Tender 60 minutes before departure, pickup 60 minutes after arrival, airport-to-airport only; the consignee (dentist or courier) must collect at the cargo building (OGG: 871 Haleakala Hwy, M-F 6:00 am-7:30 pm, Sat-Sun 6:00 am-1:00 pm; ANC and SEA GoldStreak 24/7). Alaska runs four SEA-OGG nonstops daily (first 08:07) and roughly 10+ SEA-ANC, so a case tendered by ~7 am can be in a Maui dentist's hands early afternoon HST. Known Shipper status is mandatory and Integra already holds it. Caveats: the guarantee requires pre-booking and only refunds up to 50% of the charge; the public rules tariff caps pieces at 100 lb while the website says 150 lb; prepaid only; rates rose ~$5 per minimum between January and October 2026. (4) Cutoffs matter more than rates. Nearest FedEx Office (1299 156th Ave NE) last Express pickup 4:00 pm M-F per a June 2026 aggregator; The UPS Store at Crossroads (15600 NE 8th St) 5:00-5:30 pm air pickup; the UPS Customer Center in Redmond shows conflicting open/closed status. Return legs are the trap: FedEx Ship Center Kahului last Express drop-off is noon M-F; FedEx Anchorage 2:30 pm; Lahaina OfficeMax 11:00 am; UPS Stores on Maui 5:00 pm M-F with no weekend air pickup. Return-leg list rates are lower than outbound (FedEx Priority Overnight from AK/HI 1 lb $78.45 list, ~$103 with fuel; FedEx/UPS 2Day/2DA ~$58-60). (5) Money-back guarantees: FedEx reinstated its guarantee Jan 13, 2026 only for First/Priority/Standard Overnight and 2Day A.M.; 2Day, Express Saver and Ground remain unguaranteed. UPS's guarantee covers only Next Day Air Early/NDA/NDA Saver/2DA A.M.; 2nd Day Air, 3 Day Select and Ground are suspended (UPS's own year-end notice). So the two services most likely to be used to Maui (FedEx 2Day, UPS 2nd Day Air) carry no refund if late. Default liability is $100 per package; declared value above that costs FedEx $4.95 ($100.01-300) then $1.65 per $100; UPS $5.10-5.11 then $1.70 per $100 (a $1,700 arch: ~$28-29). Damage claims must be noticed within 60 days at both carriers. Estimates: Eugene/Spokane assumed zone 3 (zone 4 possible), Dallas zone 7 (8 possible), Aliso Viejo UPS zone 5 (published from the reverse 926 chart; FedEx zone 5 assumed), Boise zone 4, Miami zone 8. UPS Hawaii-origin pages were not retrieved; Alaska-origin tables are used as proxy because FedEx and UPS AK/HI figures match to the cent. Pirate Ship publishes no AK/HI rate table ("up to 75% off Daily Rates" for air; AK/HI "may incur UPS surcharges"); the UPS Small Business Rate Guide is used as the proxy for a discounted small-shipper tier. Unknowns: whether the Maui ZIPs are FedEx "Hawaii rural" (zone 12), whether they sit on either carrier's Alaska/Hawaii delivery-area-surcharge list (FedEx $16.25 HI/$46.00 AK; UPS $16.50/$48.25), exact UPS zones from 980 (file not retrievable), the nearest FedEx Ship Center to Bellevue, and GoldStreak pricing for Boise/Eugene/Spokane (zone 9 is absent from the GoldStreak chart). Disruption record for the Hawaii lanes is real but episodic: Maui wildfire flight delays (Aug 2023), Aloha Air Cargo weather suspensions at OGG/KOA (Mar 2026), Hurricane Lala (Aug 2026), the UPS Louisville MD-11 crash (Nov 2025) that suspended guarantees, and USPS pushing Hawaii Ground Advantage to 10+ days from Oct 1, 2026.

**Findings.**

- *fact, high confidence.* FedEx 2026 list-rate footnotes restrict Express service to Alaska/Hawaii. Value: Express Saver: not to AK/HI; SO: AK metro only; 2Day AM: AK only. [source](https://mailomg.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/FedEx_Standard_List_Rates_2026.pdf) (Effective January 5, 2026; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* FedEx service guide: Priority Overnight is 2 business days and 2Day is 3 business days for 'certain shipments' to/from Alaska and Hawaii; Ground 3-7 business days. Value: PO: 2 business days (certain AK/HI); 2Day: 3 business days (certain AK/HI). [source](https://www.leegov.com/procurement/Project%20Documents/PB220082JJB%20-%20Courier%20Services/FedEx%202024%20U.S.%20Service%20Guide.pdf) (2024 guide (undated page); abcdocz Hawaii/Alaska commitment sheet undated; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* UPS offers only Next Day Air, 2nd Day Air and Ground from the 48 states to Alaska/Hawaii; zone 124/224/44 = AK/HI metro, 126/226/46 = AK/HI extended, 125/225/45 = Puerto Rico. Value: NDA Saver, 2DA A.M., 3 Day Select: not available to AK/HI. [source](https://www.ups.com/media/us/currentrates/zone-txt/926.txt) (undated (current rates); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Kahului 96732, Kihei 96753 and Lahaina 96761 are UPS 'extended' Hawaii ZIPs; Honolulu 968xx and Anchorage 995xx are metro. Value: Maui = extended; Honolulu, Anchorage = metro. [source](https://www.ups.com/assets/resources/webcontent/en_US/ak-hi_retail_rates.pdf) (2022 guide zone charts (effective Dec 27, 2021); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* UPS 2026 Daily Rates, 1 lb / 3 lb, by zone (list, before fuel). Value: see detail. [source](https://assets.ups.com/adobe/assets/urn:aaid:aem:a4030115-44a0-4935-b953-e8f65732ef9f/original/as/preview-daily-rates-us-en.pdf) (2026 Daily Rates effective Dec 22, 2025 (guide re-issued May 10, 2026); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* UPS publishes a separate 2026 'Small Business Rates' tariff that is roughly 40-60% below Daily Rates, including to AK/HI. Value: NDA 1 lb to Maui (z126): $76.34 SBR vs $127.45 Daily. [source](https://assets.ups.com/adobe/assets/urn%3Aaaid%3Aaem%3Abbf2553c-15e5-4b2d-abcd-b7445b13daa0/original/as/small-business-rate-guide-us-en.pdf) (Updated January 26, 2026; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* UPS Simple Rate (flat, no fuel surcharge) prices to Alaska/Hawaii. Value: 2DA XS box: $48.80 metro HI/AK, $90.05 remote HI/AK. [source](https://assets.ups.com/adobe/assets/urn:aaid:aem:a4030115-44a0-4935-b953-e8f65732ef9f/original/as/preview-daily-rates-us-en.pdf) (2026 Daily Rates; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* FedEx 2026 Standard List Rates, 1 lb / 3 lb (before fuel) by zone. Value: see detail. [source](https://mailomg.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/FedEx_Standard_List_Rates_2026.pdf) (Effective January 5, 2026 (mirror of fedex.com PDF, which blocks crawlers); cross-checked against quadient.com; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* FedEx 2026 list rates to Alaska/Hawaii metro (zones 9/10) and rural (11/12), and Ground to AK/HI. Value: PO 1 lb to HI/AK metro $130.21 list; 2Day $68.03; Ground $43.41. [source](https://mailomg.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/FedEx_Standard_List_Rates_2026.pdf) (Effective January 5, 2026; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* FedEx and UPS return-leg (from Alaska/Hawaii to the 48 states) list rates are lower than outbound and match each other to the cent. Value: Return PO/NDA 1 lb $78.45 list; 2Day/2DA ~$44-45. [source](https://assets.ups.com/adobe/assets/urn:aaid:aem:c7a3d067-725a-4b33-887a-586753b3e615/original/as/preview-ak-hi-rates-us-en.pdf) (UPS 2026 AK/HI Daily Rates preview (eff. Dec 22, 2025); FedEx list rates eff. Jan 5, 2026; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* FedEx One Rate (flat, fuel and DAS included) covers Alaska/Hawaii at the National zone and is the cheapest published 2-day option to Maui. Value: 2Day small box $19.55 to HI/AK; PO small box $148.20. [source](https://www.shipscience.com/wp-content/uploads/FedEx-2026-04-20-OneRate-Pricing_2026.pdf) (Effective April 20, 2026; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Fuel surcharges in force week of Oct 5-11, 2026. Value: FedEx Express 31.75% / Ground 29.25%; UPS Air 32.25% / Ground 29.75%. [source](https://www.oilpriceapi.com/fuel-surcharge/ups) (Effective October 5, 2026 (also kadimalogistics.com Oct 5, 2026; ebblogistics.com tracker); accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* 2026 Alaska/Hawaii delivery area surcharges apply to select ZIPs. Value: HI $16.25-16.50; AK $46.00-48.25 per package. [source](https://ebblogistics.com/tools/das-lookup/) (undated page citing FedEx list eff. Jul 20, 2026 and UPS rates eff. Dec 22, 2025; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* 2026 declared value charges. Value: FedEx $1.65/$100; UPS $1.70/$100 above $300. [source](https://www.darrigoconsulting.com/blog/what-does-ups-declared-value-charge-mean) (2026-01-22 (UPS); shipaid.com 2026 FedEx table; 3plcenter.com 2026-02-12; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Pirate Ship (Integra's current tool) advertises up to 75% off UPS Daily Rates on air services but publishes no AK/HI rate table and warns of AK/HI surcharges. Value: up to 75% off NDA/NDAS/2DA Daily Rates. [source](https://www.pirateship.com/ups/discounted-ups-shipping-rates) (undated; support article updated 2026-06-08; accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, high confidence.* Alaska Air Cargo GoldStreak rates effective Oct 1, 2026: SEA (zone 4) to Hawaii (zone 11) minimum $99; to Anchorage (zone 2) $74; to Orange County (zone 6) $69; to Dallas/Miami (zones 7/8) $79. Value: SEA-OGG $99 min (1-3 lb); SEA-ANC $74; SEA-SNA $69. **Unknowns the agent could not resolve.**

- UPS origin zone chart for ZIP prefix 980 (ups.com zone-txt/980.txt) could not be fetched; Eugene/Spokane (zone 3 vs 4), Dallas/Plano (7 vs 8) and Boise (4) zones are estimated; Aliso Viejo zone 5 is inferred by symmetry from the published 926 chart.
- Whether Kahului 96732, Kihei 96753 and Lahaina 96761 are FedEx 'Hawaii metro' (zone 10) or 'Hawaii rural' (zone 12); the 2026 list-rate PDF text does not include the ZIP assignment. Rural adds ~$4-30 per shipment and may change the commitment from next-day to 2nd-day.
- Whether any of the Maui ZIPs or Anchorage 99503 appear on the FedEx/UPS Alaska-Hawaii delivery area surcharge ZIP lists ($16.25-$16.50 HI; $46.00-$48.25 AK).
- Actual Pirate Ship label prices for UPS Next Day Air / 2nd Day Air to Hawaii and Alaska (no published AK/HI table; 'up to 75% off' is a ceiling). The UPS Small Business Rate Guide was used as the proxy for discounted pricing; Integra's actual Pirate Ship quotes should be pulled for 96732 and 99503 at 1 lb and 3 lb.
- UPS Hawaii-origin Daily Rate pages (return-leg Maui->Bellevue) were not retrieved; Alaska-origin figures were used as the proxy because FedEx and UPS AK/HI tables match to the cent.
- Exact FedEx Hawaii-inbound commitment per service in 2026 (the table shows next day by 12 p.m., next day by 5 p.m., 2nd day by 5 p.m. for First Overnight/Priority Overnight/2Day, with column mapping inferred); FedEx First Overnight availability to Maui ZIPs unconfirmed.
- Nearest FedEx Ship Center (Express station with late cutoff) to Bellevue 98007 was not identified; only FedEx Office (4:00 pm) and Authorized ShipCenters were found. Status of the UPS Customer Center at 18001 NE Union Hill Rd, Redmond is contradictory across listings.
- FedEx Office/OnSite Express cutoffs on Maui other than the Kahului Ship Center (12:00 pm) and Lahaina OfficeMax (11:00 am) were not captured; The UPS Store Lahaina air pickup time not captured; Honolulu return-leg locations not researched.
- GoldStreak pricing for Boise, Eugene and Spokane: these cities sit in Alaska Air Cargo zone 9, which is not a column in the GoldStreak rate chart; whether zone 4 rates apply is unknown. Local courier cost from OGG cargo to Kihei/Lahaina offices was not researched (estimated ~$30).
- Conflict between GoldStreak web copy (up to 150 lb per piece) and the published Rules tariff (pieces over 100 lb not accepted); immaterial for dental cases but noted.
- Whether the UPS Daily Rate guide re-issued May 10, 2026 changed any base rates versus the Dec 22, 2025 tables used here (mid-year re-issues usually change surcharges, not base rates).
- FedEx's own 2026 Service Guide, fuel-surcharge page, holiday schedule and One Rate pages block automated retrieval (HTTP 'System Down'); figures were taken from an exact mirror of the FedEx list-rate PDF and from consultant trackers that cite fedex.com.
- Hawaii Saturday delivery availability for the specific Maui ZIPs (FedEx states Oahu-only select PM ZIPs; UPS Saturday service on Maui not verified).
- Empirical on-time performance on the Seattle-Maui lane for FedEx 2Day/Priority Overnight and UPS Next Day Air (carriers do not publish lane-level reliability).

### B.9 Equipment Market

*Scope:* Equipment and CAM-software dealer economics for an ARUM dealer with no territory protection plus MillBox/hyperDENT reseller (Integra Dental Design, Bellevue WA)

**Agent summary.** Bottom line for a skeptical reader: the ARUM dealership, as structured (15-unit first-year commitment, no territory, one A3 demo bought at $17,500 against a ~$51,000 list), is competing in a channel that already has four US sellers of the same machines, including the manufacturer's own new subsidiary, and against competitor mills whose published street prices make the A3's list price unachievable. The realistic gross margin per compact dry mill is roughly $10,000–$20,000 before install, training, travel and warranty labor, and the realistic close volume for a six-person lab with zero closes in six months is a handful of units per year, not 15. FACTS (sourced, access date 2026-10-12). Channel: ARUM Dentistry USA Inc. is a manufacturer-owned subsidiary at 17952 Sky Park Circle, Suite 45-C, Irvine, CA (arumdentistry.com; Hinman 2026 exhibitor booth #1901), led by CEO Howard Kim since Dec 2025/Jan 2026, four employees, publicly recruiting "strong dealer partners" (LinkedIn, 2026-03-06) and hiring nationwide implant sales reps (2026-08). DECSTA (15375 Barranca Pkwy, Irvine) is an "official distributor of Arum Dentistry in the US" running Lab Day "Chicago Specials" on 5X-500/5X-300Pro (Feb 2025). Doowon USA / Dental Solution USA (Lansdale PA, Torrance CA) sells 5X-200 through 5X-500L. Axsys Dental Solutions (Wixom MI) has sold ARUM-built "Versamill" mills since at least 2014; its 5X-200 is listed at $103,600 including 3 days training in Michigan plus 2 days on-site installation and a 1-year warranty (Blue Sky Bio store, undated) and was quoted at a $94,500 US base price by Axsys on Dental Lab Network (May 2024); Axsys's PersonalCARE Gold support plan is $2,500/yr. So Integra's planned South Orange County expansion lands in the same city as the manufacturer and its official distributor. Competitor price anchors (authorized US dealer pages, undated unless noted): vhf E5 $28,990, N4+ $34,990, Z4 $39,990, K5+ $40,990, S5 $51,990, R5 $84,990 (R5 manual-load $69,990), and vhf ships dentalcam CAM with no license fee. Roland/DGSHAPE DWX-52D MSRP $25,995; DWX-52DCi $34,995 US (Roland DGA ROI calculator, which also prices CAM at $8,000 and a dust extractor at $1,995). imes-icore CORiTEC 350i PRO+ €59,450 (Dental Direkt, Nov 2023). Used anchors: vhf Z4 (2019) $32,000; inLab MC X5 (2017) $16,000; Ivoclar PM7 (2022) hammered at $19,250 + 10% premium (Sept 2023); Zirkonzahn M1 (2020) $4,250. Several "new PM7 for $17,900–$21,000" and "DWX-52D $9,200" web listings show scam-site patterns and are not price anchors. CAM software: MillBox new license $8,450 (Standard) up to ~$16,500 (Expert multi-postprocessor) with annual updates at $1,200/yr (Denteon, Elemental Dental Supply, Kodent); CIMsystem routes technical support through the reseller. hyperDENT Compact 4X $3,640 (US Dental Depot), Compact $6,900 (Kodent), an Implant Light bundle $11,230 (Dentcore); labs report hyperDENT maintenance invoices of $6,750/yr plus $2,600 support (DLN, Jan 2023) and Compact renewal quotes of $1,500–$2,500 (DLN, 2020). hyperDENT V10 replaces Classic and Compact; reseller margins are not published. Dealer margin norms: the In re Dental Supplies antitrust complaint (E.D.N.Y. 1:16-cv-00696) alleges the big distributors held equipment gross margins at or above 32% (Patterson floor 32% on equipment, 28–30% supplies, 2011–2015) while discounters ran 15–25%; these are allegations, not findings. The Sirona–Patterson CAD/CAM distributorship (SEC exhibit, 2012) shows standard obligations: demo equipment in every branch, spare-parts stock, installation capability, factory-trained technicians, and the distributor paying training costs. Market: Census CBP 2023 counts 4,782 US dental-lab establishments (42,188 employees), down 146 from 2022; CA 734, FL 424, TX 277, WA 175. Establishments fell 15.8% from 2017 to 2022. LMT's Nov 2023 poll: two-thirds of labs offer milled restorations and 45% of those do all milling in-house (about 30% of all labs); among in-house millers 67% own two or more mills. Dentists milling in-house fell from 14% (2023) to 12% (2024) while intraoral-scanner use rose to 57% (Inside Dentistry, Dec 2024). Lab Day Chicago 2026 drew 5,100+ attendees and 297 exhibitors; Lab Day West 2026 drew 1,600+ attendees and 131 exhibitors, with an educator publicly noting registration "well below" prior years. Tax and finance: for 2026 the Section 179 cap is $2,560,000 with a $4,090,000 phase-out (Rev. Proc. 2025-32) and 100% bonus depreciation is permanent for property acquired after Jan 19, 2025; the placed-in-service date governs. Dental lenders price qualified borrowers at 6–9% with 36–84 month terms; vendors are paid after a signed delivery/acceptance (Geneva Capital), with next-day funding and a 100% prefunding option for qualified vendors (Ascentium). ESTIMATES: A3 street price $28,000–$38,000 (bracketed by DWX-52D/E5 below and 52DCi/Z4 above), yielding $10,500–$20,500 gross and roughly $6,000–$15,000 contribution after ~$3,500 install/training/travel, ~$600 freight and a ~$1,000 warranty reserve. Demo-unit carrying cost ~$5,000/yr. Sales cycle 4–12 months with 8–12 touches and ~20–25% demo-to-close (manufacturing benchmarks). UNKNOWNS: ARUM's dealer price list beyond the A3, ARUM USA's direct-sale policy and whether it will undercut dealers, warranty-labor reimbursement, LMT booth rates, OR/HI/AK lab counts, share of labs with 5-axis mills, and MillBox/hyperDENT reseller discounts.

**Findings.**

- *fact, high confidence.* ARUM Dentistry has its own US subsidiary in Irvine, CA (ARUM Dentistry USA Inc.) that is actively recruiting dealers and building a direct sales team. Value: ARUM USA: 4 employees, Irvine CA, est. Dec 2025/Jan 2026. [source](https://arumdentistry.com/eng/about/location.php) (undated (LinkedIn posts dated 2026-01-10, 2026-03-06, 2026-08-20))
- *fact, high confidence.* At least three other US companies sell ARUM machines: Axsys (Versamill), DECSTA (Irvine) and Doowon USA/Dental Solution USA. Value: 4+ US sellers of ARUM machines incl. manufacturer subsidiary. [source](https://lmtmag.com/digitalstomusa) (undated (DECSTA Lab Day posts Feb 19-20, 2025))
- *fact, high confidence.* Axsys Versamill 5X-200 (ARUM 5X-200 derivative) is priced at $94,500–$103,600 in the US, and the price includes 3 days of factory training plus 2 days of on-site installation. Value: $94,500–$103,600; +$2,500/yr Gold support after year 1. [source](https://blueskybio.com/store/versamill-5x200) (undated; DLN table edited 2024-05-15)
- *historical, medium confidence.* Historical ARUM/Versamill US price points for smaller models: 5X-300 ~$38,000 (2019 quote), 5X-450 'just under $60K' (Axsys, ~2019-2020), a 2014-era Versamill described by its owner as an '$85,000 machine'. Value: 5X-300 ~$38K (2019); 5X-450 <$60K (2019-20). [source](https://dentallabnetwork.com/forums/threads/coritec-250i-vs-arum-5x-300-for-small-lab.29794/page-2) (2019-06-16 (thread), 2022-01-25 (thread))
- *estimate, medium confidence.* No US dealer publishes an A3/A3L/A5 PRO/A7 price; all are quote-based. The given $51,000 list vs $17,500 dealer cost implies a 2.9x list markup that competitor street prices will not support. Value: A3 realistic street $28K–$38K; gross $10.5K–$20.5K. [source](https://arumdentistry.com/eng/solution/aseries4.php) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* vhf US authorized-dealer prices (2026 pages): E3 $20,990; E4 $24,990; E5 $28,990; N4+ $34,990; Z4 $39,990; K5+ $40,990; S5 $51,990; R5 $84,990; R5 with manual blank change $69,990 — and vhf includes its dentalcam CAM software with no license fee. Value: vhf E5 $28,990; S5 $51,990; R5 $84,990. [source](https://dentcore.com/products/vhf-r5) (undated (price valid through 2027-10-08 per Elemental schema))
- *fact, high confidence.* Roland/DGSHAPE: DWX-52D MSRP $25,995; DWX-52DCi $34,995 (US), with Roland's own ROI calculator budgeting CAM software at $8,000 and a dust extractor at $1,995. Value: DWX-52D $25,995; DWX-52DCi $34,995; CAM $8,000. [source](https://www.rolanddga.com/applications/dental-cad-cam/dwx52dci-roi-calculator) (undated (press release 2019-05-29))
- *fact, medium confidence.* imes-icore CORiTEC pricing anchors are European: 350i PRO+ €59,450 and 350i Loader PRO+ €65,283–€69,450 ex-VAT; US Dental Depot stocks CORiTEC in the US but does not publish prices and notes 4–6 week German parts lead times. Value: 350i PRO+ €59,450; Loader PRO+ €65,283–69,450. [source](https://shop.dentaldirekt.de/en/products/milling-machine-coritec-350i-pro-serie) (2023-11-20)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Ivoclar PrograMill PM7, Dentsply inLab MC X5, Zirkonzahn and Amann Girrbach do not publish US list prices; credible used/auction anchors: PM7 (2022) $19,250 + 10% buyer premium at auction (Sept 2023); inLab MC X5 (2017) $16,000; Zirkonzahn M1 (2020) $4,250; Sirona MC XL used $8,075 (Aug 2026). Value: PM7 used $19,250+10%; MC X5 used $16,000. [source](https://auction.rosensystems.com/auctions/10067/lot/28357-dental-milling-system-ivoclar-vivadent-2022-progra-mill-pm7-s-n-9105001091-with-suction-unit-1-ingersoll-rand-mdl-d25in-sr-15-cfm-1-hp-z2-pc-core-i7-with-phillips-monitor-hp-keyboard-and-mouse) (auction closed 2023-09-15)
- *historical, medium confidence.* Large dental distributors have historically targeted ~32% gross margin on equipment (28–30% on supplies); discounters operated at 15–25%; these figures come from a class-action complaint and are allegations, not adjudicated facts. Value: alleged 32% equipment margin floor; discounters 15–25%. [source](https://storage.courtlistener.com/recap/gov.uscourts.nyed.381357/gov.uscourts.nyed.381357.49.0.pdf) (filed 2016-03-11)
- *fact, high confidence.* Typical CAD/CAM distributor obligations (from a public Sirona–Patterson agreement): stock product and spare parts, keep demo equipment in showrooms/training centers, maintain installation capability and factory-trained technicians, and bear training costs in the distributor's lab market. Value: demo units + parts stock + install + trained techs + training costs on distributor. [source](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1014507/000119312512261786/d363113dex102.htm) (2012 (exhibit filed 2012-06))
- *fact, high confidence.* MillBox US pricing: new license $8,450 (Standard) to about $16,500 (Expert multi-postprocessor); annual updates $1,200/yr with a 3-year ($3,600) cap to reinstate lapsed licenses; a new license includes the current year plus one year of updates. Value: $8,450–$16,500 license; $1,200/yr updates. [source](https://denteon.com/products/millbox-dental-cam) (undated (Elemental price valid through 2027-09-26))
- *fact, high confidence.* hyperDENT US pricing: Compact 4X + PP + 1 year $3,640; Compact $6,900; Implant Light bundle (Compact 5X + maintenance + implant module + dongle + tools) $11,230; labs report annual maintenance invoices of $6,750 (full CAM, via Axsys, 2023) and $1,500–$2,500 for Compact (2020). Value: $3,640–$11,230 licenses/bundles; maintenance $1.5K–$6.75K/yr. [source](https://usdentaldepot.com/hyperdent-compact-4x-pp-1-year-hyperdent-x4-2) (undated (DLN threads 2023-01-03 and 2020-01-30))
- *fact, high confidence.* US dental laboratories: 4,782 establishments with 42,188 employees in 2023 (Census CBP), down 146 establishments and 351 employees from 2022; establishments fell 15.8% from 2017 to 2022. Value: 4,782 labs (2023); WA 175; CA 734; TX 277; FL 424. [source](https://plainbizbench.com/industry/339116/) (CBP 2023 (released June 2025); page updated Aug 2026)
- *estimate, medium confidence.* Roughly 30% of US labs mill in-house (two-thirds offer milled restorations and 45% of those do all milling in-house), and two-thirds of in-house millers own two or more mills; the share owning a 5-axis mill specifically is not published. Value: ~30% of labs mill in-house; 67% of those have 2+ mills. [source](https://www.lmtmag.com/articles/milling-market-facts) (2023-11-17)
- *fact, high confidence.* Dentist in-office milling is small and flat-to-declining: 12% of surveyed dentists milled restorations in-house in 2024 (14% in 2023), while intraoral scanner use jumped to 57%. Value: 12% of dentists mill in-house (2024); 57% use IOS. [source](https://insidedentistry.net/2024/12/trends-in-dentistry-2024/) (2024-12-01)
- *fact, high confidence.* 2026 Section 179: $2,560,000 deduction cap, phase-out beginning at $4,090,000 (fully phased out at $6,650,000); 100% bonus depreciation is permanent for qualifying property acquired after January 19, 2025; the placed-in-service date determines the tax year. Value: $2,560,000 / $4,090,000; 100% bonus permanent. [source](https://www.usbank.com/corporate-and-commercial-banking/insights/credit-finance/equipment/maximize-deductions-section-179.html) (undated (Harness article 2026-07-27; CALT 2025-07-09))
- *fact, high confidence.* Dental equipment financing norms (2026): qualified established borrowers 6–9% at specialty lenders, 7.5–18% overall range, 36–84 month terms, 100% financing (including soft costs) common for established practices, 10–20% down for startups; captive/manufacturer programs run periodic 0% promos. Value: 6–9% qualified; 36–84 mo; 0–20% down. [source](https://dentalpracticeinsider.org/dental-equipment-financing/) (2026-06-29)
- *fact, high confidence.* Vendor-pay-at-acceptance is the norm: lenders pay the dealer after delivery is confirmed and the customer signs a delivery/acceptance form; funding is typically next-day to 48 hours after signed documents, and some lenders offer 100% prefunding to qualified vendors. Value: paid after signed D&A; 24–48 hr; prefunding available. [source](https://gogc.com/lease-process/) (2021-12-08 (vendor page 2025-05-19))
- *fact, high confidence.* LMT Lab Day attendance: Chicago 2026 drew 5,100+ attendees and 297 exhibitors (6,096 registered); Lab Day West 2026 drew 1,600+ attendees and 131 exhibitors (1,968 registered), down from 1,765 attendees/~140 exhibitors in 2024 and 1,700+/156 in 2023. Value: Chicago 5,100+/297; West 1,600+/131. [source](https://www.lmtmag.com/articles/lab-day-chicago-2026-the-industry-s-biggest-weekend-delivers-big-time) (2026-02-23 (West article 2026-05-19))
- *estimate, medium confidence.* Sales-cycle benchmarks for $25K–$100K capital equipment: ~120 days seller-visible cycle (manufacturing), dental capital equipment 6–12 months with 8–12 touchpoints, demo-to-close ~20–25%; no dental-lab-mill-specific close-rate data exists publicly. Value: ~120 days visible cycle; 8–12 touches; 20–25% demo-to-close. [source](https://www.supplyco.ai/blog/industrial-equipment-sales-cycle-guide) (2025-06-18 (Pulse RevOps 2026-06-26))
- *historical, medium confidence.* Trade-show lead economics (CEIR): an exhibition-sourced prospect costs ~$96 to meet vs ~$1,039 in the field and ~$2,188 to close vs ~$3,102; 54% of exhibition-initiated sales close in 3 or fewer calls; ~39% of captured leads qualify; cost per trade-show lead benchmarks range $283 (Exhibit Surveys, older) to $811 (2024). Value: $2,188 to close an exhibition lead; CPL $283–$811. [source](https://www.exhibitoronline.com/topics/article.asp?ID=1224) (undated (CEIR study; Industry Insights 2015))
- *fact, high confidence.* Install-travel cost basis: GSA FY2026 standard per diem is $110 lodging + $68 M&IE = $178/day (California average lodging $167.52, M&IE $86); BTS average domestic itinerary fare Q2 2026 $436–$445 (Seattle origin $453.69, Los Angeles $499.60). Value: $178/day per diem; ~$450 SEA round trip. [source](https://www.gsa.gov/policy-regulations/regulations/federal-travel-regulation/ftr-and-related-files/gsa-per-diem-bulletin-ftr-2601) (FY2026 (effective 2025-10-01); BTS Q2 2026 release 2026-09)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Spindle and service cost anchors: Jäger replacement spindles for Amann Girrbach Motion 2 list $5,133–$6,665 (sale $4,492–$5,831); Roland DWX-50 spindle ~$2,000–$2,300; Ivoclar Zenotec Mini spindle $2,700 new / $1,800 rebuilt; Axsys quotes $2,000–$3,000 for an on-site re-homing service call and ~3-month spindle lead time (2022); Sycotec 3 kW spindle price not published. Value: spindles $2,000–$6,665; service call $2,000–$3,000. [source](https://usdentaldepot.com/jager-spindle-m2i-amann-girrbach-179323) (undated (DLN threads 2015-01-12, 2021-12-15, 2022-01-25))
- *fact, medium confidence.* ARUM reputation among US labs is mixed: praised for metal-milling rigidity and value versus Datron/DCS-class machines, but with documented complaints about calibration drift, probe reliability, spindle lead times, encoder-battery crashes, support fees and channel confusion between Axsys-modified Versamills and ARUM-direct machines. Value: mixed; calibration/support complaints documented 2017–2024. [source](https://dentallabnetwork.com/forums/threads/arum-versamill-5x-software-help.33294/) (2022-01-25 (other threads 2017-06-29, 2021-02-11, 2022-11-14, 2024-09-17))
- *estimate, medium confidence.* Per-machine economics (estimate): an A3 sold at $32,000 yields ~$14,500 gross and ~$9,000–$10,000 contribution after dealer-absorbed install, training, travel, freight and warranty reserve; at 3–5 units/yr the dealership contributes ~$30,000–$50,000 before demo carrying, trade-show and marketing costs of ~$15,000–$25,000/yr. Value: ~$9K–$10K contribution per A3; $30K–$50K/yr at 3–5 units. [source](https://dentcore.com/products/vhf-r5) (undated; inputs accessed 2026-10-12)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Lab consolidation is real but the industry remains fragmented; M&A activity is re-accelerating in 2026. Value: 18 deals (2021); 8 through Jun 2026; top-50 = 46% revenue. [source](https://verticaliq.com/product/dental-laboratories/) (undated (page content references June 2026))

**Targets named.**

- **ARUM Dentistry USA Inc. (principal) — renegotiation target** (other; Irvine, CA). The 15-unit commitment and lack of territory are the core economic risks; ARUM USA is publicly recruiting dealers (Mar 2026) and expanding direct sales, so dealer protection, demo-unit support, warranty-labor reimbursement and show co-op funding should be negotiated before further investment. Decision-maker: Howard Kim, CEO, ARUM Dentistry USA Inc.. Reach: 949-340-2971; hjkim626@arumdentistry.com (listed on arumdentistry.com); Hinman/Lab Day exhibitor contacts. [source](https://arumdentistry.com/eng/about/worldwide.php?b_cate=34)
- **Geneva Capital LLC (vendor finance partner)** (other; Alexandria, MN (national niche lender; exhibited at Lab Day West 2026 and Lab Day Chicago 2024)). Dental-lab-focused direct lender used by Axsys; offers vendor program with approvals to $250K, $0-down structures, show-floor approvals, and pays the vendor after signed delivery form — solves Integra's cash-poor position when a customer buys a $30K+ mill. Decision-maker: Vendor relations team (dedicated 4–5 person vendor PODs). Reach: gogc.com/vendors; present at LMT Lab Day booths. [source](https://gogc.com/vendors/)
- **Ascentium Capital (vendor program with 100% prefunding option)** (other; Kingwood, TX (national)). Offers qualified vendors 100% prefunding and next-day funding, application-only to $250K, up to 84 months and no down payment — relevant if Integra must pay ARUM for a machine before the customer's lender funds. Decision-maker: Vendor program sales. Reach: ascentiumcapital.com/offer-financing; 866-722-8500. [source](https://ascentiumcapital.com/offer-financing)
- **In-house milling labs in WA/OR/CA with 2+ mills (upgrade/replacement cycle)** (dental lab; Washington (175 lab establishments), Oregon (count unknown), California (734)). LMT 2023: 67% of in-house millers own 2+ mills; replacement/expansion buyers are the realistic ARUM prospects (metal-capable A5 PRO/A7 vs zirconia A3), and Lab Day West (Garden Grove) is the regional gathering (1,600+ attendees). Decision-maker: Lab owner / CAD-CAM manager. Reach: LMT Lab Day West exhibiting, LMTmag lab directory, Medit Link/3Shape Communicate connections, lab-to-lab milling customers (Bear Milling) as the demo funnel. [source](https://www.lmtmag.com/articles/milling-market-facts)

Dec 2025/Jan 2026, CEO Howard Kim, 4 employees) selling ARUM mills, NB implants, zirconia, resins and premills; exhibited at Hinman 2026 (booth #1901); recruiting dealers and hiring nationwide implant sales reps Pricing: Not published; A3L list ~$51,000 (given); A3 dealer cost $17,500 (given) Turnaround: n/a Direct competitor/channel conflict risk in the exact Orange County market Integra is considering.
