**Unknowns the agent could not resolve.**

- ARUM's US dealer price list for A3L, A3 PRO, A5 PRO, A7/A7L and 5X-500 series (only the A3 dealer cost of $17,500 and ~$51,000 A3L list are known from context); whether ARUM USA sells direct to labs and at what price relative to dealer street price
- ARUM dealer agreement terms beyond the 15-unit commitment: required demo inventory, warranty-labor reimbursement rates, parts-stocking requirements, co-op marketing/show funding, payment terms (deposit vs pay-on-shipment), and whether prefunding by a lender is accepted
- LMT Lab Day Chicago and West exhibitor booth rates (behind exhibitor-portal login); estimate used in cost_items
- Census CBP 2019 state baselines and 2023 counts for Oregon, Hawaii and Alaska dental labs (NAICS 339116); Census API requires a key and data.census.gov tables are JS-rendered; Hawaii DBEDT posts CBP files but as spreadsheets not read here
- Share of US labs owning a 5-axis mill specifically (NADL Materials & Equipment Surveys 2022–2025 are paywalled); only the ~30% in-house-milling share (LMT 2023) and 2+ mill ownership distribution are public
- MillBox (CIMsystem) and hyperDENT (FOLLOW-ME!) reseller discount/margin schedules and minimums; whether MillBox OEM 'Eco' licenses bundled with ARUM mills carry reseller margin
- Dental-lab-mill-specific close rates, demos per close and average sales-cycle length; only cross-industry capital-equipment and dental-dealer staffing benchmarks were found
- Current US price of a Sycotec 3 kW (5X-200/A7-class) or 0.8 kW (A3-class) replacement spindle and ARUM's parts price list
- Whether the 2016 dental-supplies antitrust allegations about 32% equipment margins reflect current practice at large distributors; small lab-equipment dealer margins (20–35%) remain an estimate
- Actual booth-level or exhibitor-level outcomes at Lab Day for mill sellers (leads per show, closes per show); LMT publishes attendance but not exhibitor results
- 2026 Section 179/bonus conformity in Washington (no income tax, n/a), California (does not conform to bonus; CA Section 179 limit remains $25,000) and Oregon — state treatment not researched here
- Whether ARUM USA's Hinman 2026 and Lab Day presence included direct machine sales to labs/clinics in Southern California (would confirm channel conflict)

### B.10 Critique

*Scope:* 

**Agent summary.** 

### B.11 National Competition

*Scope:* National competition for Integra Dental Design: dentist-facing shipping labs (Glidewell, Dandy, NDX, DDS, Modern/MicroDental, ROE, etc.), lab-to-lab milling centers (Monster, Alien, Haus, Argen, Strategy, etc.), dentist lab-switching evidence, and the DSO in-house/preferred-lab trend

**Agent summary.** Method note: the shared WebSearch budget was exhausted before this task started, so discovery ran on Exa search and all primary pages were read with Exa fetch. All access dates are 2026-10-12; page dates are given where shown. FACTS, most consequential first. 1. Glidewell is the price and speed anchor, and it undercuts Integra on every commodity line. Glidewell's live price list (glidewell.com/price-list, undated, accessed 2026-10-12) shows BruxZir Full-Strength at $109 analog / $89 model-free digital, BruxZir Esthetic $119/$99, e.max posterior $129/$109, titanium custom abutment $318/$298, BruxZir crown + custom abutment $454 (minus $40 digital = $414), BruxZir screw-retained $339 (minus $20 digital = $319), and three digital full-arch tiers: Complete Service $2,795 (Esthetic $2,995), Copy Service $1,695, and direct Mill Service from a provided file $1,450 (Esthetic $1,650). Analog full-arch is $3,433, or $4,150 with a titanium bar; the screw-retained hybrid denture (milled Ti bar + denture) is $2,335; a milled implant bar/framework is $923. Monolithic BruxZir is 3 days in lab; Next-Day BruxZir (digital, scan by 2 p.m.) includes free shipping. Shipping otherwise is FedEx overnight $9 each way per box or free USPS Priority (2–4 days). BruxZir carries a lifetime warranty with a no-fault remake policy, and Glidewell will replace any other lab's fractured monolithic zirconia crown free. Against this, Integra's $129 FCZ is 45% above Glidewell's digital price, its $149 e.max is 37% above, and its $269–$299 implant-crown package is roughly at parity with Glidewell's $319 digital screw-retained price. Integra's $1,700 monolithic arch is $1,095 below Glidewell's Complete Service but only $250 above Glidewell's dentist-facing mill-from-file price. 2. Dandy sells a scanner-plus-lab subscription, not a fee schedule. Its pricing page (meetdandy.com/pricing) states a $1,000/month lab minimum for a free Dandy Vision scanner or $2,500/month for a TRIOS 5, month-to-month, free remakes, lifetime warranty on fixed work, free shipping, and a DSO tier with a free scanner per location. Per-unit fees are only partially public: zirconia "from $99," dentures from $199/arch, surgical guides $109/site, night guards from $59 bundled (Dandy labs page), and $398 list for an all-in implant restoration (Dandy implant page, offer dated to April 30, 2026). A third-party review (Peak Dental Studio, May 2026) reports e.max from $125. Fixed work is made in Utah, removables in Texas; Dandy accepts no physical impressions. 3. Lab-to-lab milling is a $19–$35 zirconia crown market with free shipping at low thresholds, so Bear Milling's $22 is at parity, not a discount. Monster Milling (Plano TX) lists Honorzir HT+ crowns at $19, implant crowns $35, Ti custom abutments from $65, a flat $350 Ti bar, full-arch zirconia from $580, Bar+Zir $950, 1-day crown milling from STL, no minimum, and free ground shipping on orders of $90+. Alien Milling (Glendora CA) lists Alien HT solid zirconia at $22, Multi-Layer 2.0 $34, implant crown $48, a flat $399 Ti bar, and full-arch hybrid zirconia at $649 (HT) to $1,049. Haus (Carrollton TX) lists crowns at $18–$35. Bear's $650 full-arch from STL sits against Monster $580, Alien $649 and K&A/Backup $650; Bear's $299 Ti bar is below Alien $399 and Monster $350 (which adds $280–$400 if Monster designs). 4. Switching behavior is well documented and stable. The LMT/Dental Economics dentist survey (LMT, Mar 2019) found 46% of dentists switched their primary lab in the prior five years, 71% because of inconsistent work, with price a factor for only about a third, and 44% found their new lab via a colleague referral. LMT's top-10 chart shows inconsistent quality 68%, poor communication 30%, late cases 26%, Rx not followed 21%, lower fees 5%. ADA's 2008 lab survey found the average GP uses three labs (90.3% use two or more; 2.8% have in-office labs). Dentaltown's 2023 poll (n=165) found 56% use two or fewer labs, 37% three to five, and 38% fired a lab in the prior 12 months. LMT's 2024 Remake Survey puts average external remakes at 2.5% (C&B) and 2.2% (implants), with labs attributing 82% of remakes to dentist error. 5. DSO consolidation redirects lab spend toward preferred networks, not toward in-house C&B labs. ADA HPI data (via IDT, Oct 2025) show DSO affiliation rising from 7.2% (2015) to 16.1% (2024); US labs with payroll fell 25.8% from 6,865 (2013) to 5,096 (2023); NADL's Spring 2025 survey attributes 65% of lab sales to solo practices and only 8% to DSOs. Heartland runs "Diamond Trusted Labs" (Leixir through 2029, DDS Lab since 2008) and reports remakes under 3% and roughly $190/arch savings versus local labs; Aspen operates onsite denture labs and trained 1,300+ technicians (Feb 2025). Glidewell opened a DSO-only BruxZir Express lab in Memphis (Apr 2025). Local to Integra: NDX Issaquah (640 NW Gilman Blvd) and Modern Dental Center Seattle (330 112th Ave NE, Bellevue) are national-network labs in Integra's own market. ESTIMATES. GP lab spend: ADA HPI 2025 gross billings average $965,660; HPI does not publish the lab-expense category, and third-party benchmarks range 5–10% of collections, implying roughly $40k–$80k/year per GP owner. Chairside mill penetration: no authoritative 2025 figure; Dentaltown 2023 self-selected poll shows 23% "already provide crowns in office"; digital impression use was 57% in 2024 (IDT). Working estimate 10–20% of GPs own a mill. UNKNOWNS. Dandy's full fee schedule, NDX/Modern/MicroDental current pricing, Burbank/Sun/Bayshore/ProSmile/Dental Lab Direct/Absolute fee schedules, and Argen/Core3d/Jensen/Zahn/Preat/Panthera/Zirkonzahn milling prices are quote- or login-only. The Harmony Dental "left Dandy" story is Integra's own account and was not independently verified; Dandy's public record is mixed (Dentaltown 2025 summary; 3.6/5 on 63 Google reviews for the Lehi lab) rather than uniformly negative.

**Findings.**

- *fact, high confidence.* 46% of dentists switched their primary lab in the prior five years; inconsistent quality was the #1 reason (71%), price a distant second (~1/3). Value: 46% switched in 5 yrs; 71% inconsistent work; ~33% price; 44% referral. [source](https://www.lmtmag.com/articles/why-dentists-switch-laboratories-102773) (2019-03-05)
- *historical, high confidence.* LMT 'Top 10 reasons dentists switch laboratories' chart: inconsistent quality 68%, poor relationship/communication 30%, delayed turnaround 26%, Rx not followed 21%, lower fees only 5%. Value: 68% / 30% / 26% / 21% / 5%. [source](https://lmtmag.com/static/pdfs/10_reasons.pdf) (undated)
- *historical, medium confidence.* 61% of dentists had switched labs at least once in the prior 10 years (2011); poor occlusion was the most common technical problem. Value: 61% switched in 10 yrs. [source](https://www.lmtmag.com/articles/why_dentists_switch_labs) (2011-04-28)
- *historical, medium confidence.* 49% of dentists changed labs in five years (2013); 56% consistently use three labs, only 13% rely on one; 17% had a digital impression system; 63% neither had nor planned chairside CAD/CAM. Value: 49% switched; 56% use 3 labs; 13% use 1 lab; 17% IOS. [source](https://www.dentistryiq.com/dentistry/restorative-cosmetic-and-whitening/article/16367174/why-dentists-are-changing-laboratories) (2013-04-01)
- *historical, high confidence.* ADA 2008 Survey on the Use of Dental Labs: average dentist uses 3 labs (range 1–9); 90.3% use two or more; 77.8% use 2–4; 12.5% use 5+; 2.8% have an in-office lab. Value: avg 3 labs; 90.3% use >=2; 2.8% in-office lab. [source](http://dentallabs.org/media/2017/06/ADA-Survey-on-Dental-Labs.pdf) (2008)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Dentaltown poll (Aug 19–Sep 20, 2023): 56% of dentists use two or fewer labs regularly, 37% three to five, 7% more than five; 38% fired or stopped using a lab in the past 12 months. Value: 56% <=2 labs; 38% fired a lab in 12 mo; 23% in-office crowns. [source](https://www.dentaltown.com/poll/results/531/labs) (2023-09-20)
- *historical, medium confidence.* Dentaltown 2003 poll (historical, n≈360–547): lab expense as % of total overhead clustered at 7–10%; 60% cited inconsistent work as the primary reason for dropping a lab, 22% poor fit; 41% chose their lab for quality vs 3% for price. Value: lab = 7–10% of overhead (58% of respondents); 60% drop labs for inconsistent work. [source](https://www.dentaltown.com/poll/results/20/what-percent-of-your-total-overhead-expense-is-your-lab-expense) (2003-10-06)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Dandy 2022 Dental Industry Report: most practices partner with 2–3 labs; 84% use labs that specialize in specific products; 42% have lab relationships of 6+ years, 22% of 10+ years; self-reported remake rate with primary lab: 49% <5%, 21% 5–9%, 17% 10–14%, 12% 15–20%, 1% >20%. Value: 2–3 labs typical; 42% relationships 6+ yrs; 70% report remakes <10%. [source](https://go.meetdandy.com/rs/103-HKO-179/images/Dandy_2022DentalIndustryReport.pdf) (2022)
- *fact, low confidence.* Dandy survey of 137 US dentists (Q3 2025, published Mar 2026): 94% have ordered a remake; 23% have remade a prosthetic 3+ times; 58% have or suspect they lost a patient due to a poor-fitting restoration. Value: 94% remakes; 58% lost a patient; 34% impression/scan error vs 31% lab error. [source](https://www.meetdandy.com/newsroom/survey-poor-fitting-prosthetics/) (2026-03-26)
- *fact, high confidence.* LMT 2024 Remake Survey: average external remake rate by department: C&B 2.5%, implants 2.2%, complete dentures 2.8%, partials 3.6%, orthodontics 3.0%. Value: C&B 2.5%; implants 2.2%; 82% attributed to dentist error. [source](https://lmtmag.com/articles/operations) (2026-02-23)
- *fact, medium confidence.* LMT lab poll (Nov 2023): two-thirds of labs offer milled restorations; of those, 45% scan and mill in-house, 34% own a scanner and outsource milling, 15% outsource the entire case, 6% outsource some milling. Value: 34% of labs outsource milling; 15% outsource whole case. [source](https://www.lmtmag.com/articles/milling-market-facts) (2023-11-17)
- *fact, high confidence.* ADA HPI Survey of Dental Practice 2025: average gross billings per private-practice GP $965,660; average GP net income $215,320 (specialists $1,213,040 / $346,520). Value: $965,660 gross billings/GP; $215,320 net income. [source](https://www.ada.org/resources/research/health-policy-institute/dental-practice-research) (2025 data (page undated))
- *estimate, medium confidence.* ESTIMATE: a typical GP owner spends roughly $40,000–$80,000 per year ($3,400–$6,700/month) on outside lab work. Value: $40k–$80k/yr per GP owner. [source](https://reciprocityaccounting.com/blog/dental-practice-overhead-percentage) (2026-09-02)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Digital impression (IOS) use among surveyed dentists rose to 57% in 2024 from 48% in 2023, ~50% in 2022 and 37% in 2020. Value: 57% IOS use (2024). [source](https://www.insidedentaltech.com/2025/02/trends-in-dentistry-2025/) (2025-02-01)
- *estimate, low confidence.* ESTIMATE: 10–20% of US general dentists own a chairside mill; no authoritative 2025 figure was found. Value: 10–20% (estimate). [source](https://www.dentalproductsreport.com/view/high-tech-takeover-the-2024-dental-products-report-technology-census-reveals-the-start-of-the-industry-s-true-shift-to-digital-solutions-) (2024-08-02)
- *fact, high confidence.* DSO affiliation of US dentists rose from 7.2% (2015) to 16.1% (2024), and 30.7% of dentists 0–5 years out of school; US dental labs with payroll fell 25.8% from 6,865 (2013) to 5,096 (2023). Value: 16.1% DSO-affiliated (2024); labs -25.8% 2013–2023. [source](https://insidedentaltech.com/2025/10/thriving-in-times-of-change/) (2025-10-01)
- *fact, medium confidence.* NADL Spring 2025 member survey: 65% of US dental lab sales come from solo practices, 24% from small group practices, only 8% from DSOs/large groups. Value: 65% solo / 24% small group / 8% DSO. [source](https://insidedentaltech.com/2025/10/thriving-in-times-of-change/) (2025-10-01)
- *fact, high confidence.* Heartland Dental (1,800+ offices, 3,000+ doctors) routes lab spend through 'Diamond Trusted Labs': 3 lab networks plus 6 single-location labs, 2 high-end aesthetic labs and 1 ortho lab; claims remakes under 3%, ~1 week in-lab turnaround, 40%+ savings for newly affiliated practices. Value: remakes <3%; ~$190/arch savings vs local labs. [source](https://heartland.com/diamond-trusted-supplies-and-labs/) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* Aspen Dental (1,100+ locations) fabricates or repairs dentures in onsite labs and trained 1,300+ lab technicians for its Signature Elite denture rollout (Feb 2025); its in-house capacity is denture-focused, not crown-and-bridge. Value: 1,300+ in-house lab techs; onsite denture labs. [source](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/aspen-dental-and-ivoclar-launch-signature-elite-denture-setting-a-new-standard-for-patient-care-302376151.html) (2025-02-13)
- *fact, high confidence.* Glidewell opened BruxZir Express (Memphis, TN), a DSO-exclusive lab offering BruxZir crowns/bridges and occlusal appliances at special low rates, 3 days in lab, free-shipping options in the contiguous US and a 100% no-fault remake policy. Value: 3 days in lab; DSO-only pricing. [source](https://www.groupdentistrynow.com/dso-group-blog/lab-cost-savings-for-dsos/) (2025-04-04)
- *fact, high confidence.* Glidewell dentist pricing (live price list): BruxZir Full-Strength $109 / $89 digital model-free; BruxZir Esthetic $119/$99; e.max posterior $129/$109, anterior $149/$129; Ti custom abutment $318/$298; BruxZir crown with custom abutment $454 (-$40 digital); BruxZir screw-retained $339 (-$20 digital). Value: FCZ $89 digital; e.max $109 digital; SR implant crown $319 digital. [source](https://glidewell.com/price-list) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* Glidewell full-arch dentist pricing: Digital BruxZir Full-Arch Complete Service $2,795 (Esthetic $2,995); Copy Service $1,695; direct Mill Service from provided file $1,450 (Esthetic $1,650); analog Complete Service $3,433, or $4,150 with titanium bar; screw-retained hybrid denture with milled Ti bar $2,335; milled implant bar/framework $923. Value: $2,795 digital complete; $1,450 mill-only; $4,150 with Ti bar. [source](https://glidewell.com/price-list) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* Glidewell shipping: FedEx Overnight $9 each way ($18 round trip per box, contiguous US) or free prepaid USPS Priority labels (2–4 business days each way); bundling multiple cases in one box waives inbound charges for additional cases; digital submission eliminates inbound shipping. Value: $9/box each way FedEx; free USPS. [source](https://glidewelldental.com/send-case/pickup-and-delivery) (2026-05-05)
- *fact, high confidence.* Glidewell warranty: BruxZir restorations (including over implants) and custom abutments warrantied for the life of the patient; screw-retained crowns/bridges and milled implant bars 7 years; dentures 1 year; no-fault remake within warranty period; Glidewell replaces any other lab's fractured single-unit monolithic zirconia crown free with BruxZir. Value: lifetime BruxZir; 7 yr bars/SR. [source](https://glidewelldental.com/policies-and-warranties) (2025-09)
- *fact, high confidence.* Dandy pricing model: $1,000/month lab minimum for a free Dandy Vision scanner ('$30K value'), $2,500/month for a TRIOS 5 ('$39K value'); month-to-month; shortfall is billed as the difference; free remakes; lifetime warranty on fixed products; free shipping; 100% digital (no physical impressions); fixed work made in Utah, removables in Texas. Value: $1,000/mo min; zirconia from $99; implant crown $398; 5-day crowns. [source](https://www.meetdandy.com/pricing/) (undated (page shows 2024-06-27 publish))
- *fact, medium confidence.* Dandy's reputation is mixed, not uniformly negative: Dentaltown's 2025 thread summary says it suits high-volume generalists (free scanner, reliable fits, live support) but critics cite scanner lock-in, limited STL export, rigid contracts/aggressive sales, mediocre esthetics and loss of lab choice; Google rating for the Lehi UT lab is 3.6/5 on 63 reviews. Value: Google 3.6/5 (63 reviews); mixed forum sentiment. [source](https://www.dentaltown.com/magazine/article/9622/dandy-lab-scanner-review) (2025-07-01)
- *fact, medium confidence.* Dandy's 2026 comparison table (its own marketing) lists standard crown turnaround including outbound shipping as Dandy 5 days, Glidewell 5 days, NDX 6 days, ROE 5 days, Keating 7 days, DDS Lab 12 days; warranties: Dandy lifetime all C&B, Glidewell lifetime zirconia only, NDX lifetime Verotek FCZ only, ROE 5-year zirconia, Keating 3-year, DDS Lab 1-year. Value: 5 / 5 / 6 / 5 / 7 / 12 days. [source](https://www.meetdandy.com/learning-center/articles/2026-dental-lab-comparison-guide/) (undated (2026))
- *fact, high confidence.* Lab-to-lab zirconia crown mill-only pricing clusters at $18–$35 with free shipping at low thresholds: Monster Milling $19 (Honorzir HT+), Alien Milling $22 (Alien HT) / $34 (Multi-Layer 2.0), Haus Milling $18 (3M Lava Esthetic) / $35 (Haus ML Pro), K&A/Backup Milling $25–$29. Value: $18–$35 per crown; Monster free ground >= $90. [source](https://monstermilling.com/collections/zirconia) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* Lab-to-lab full-arch zirconia from STL: Monster $580 (Honorzir) / $770 (Aizir ML) / $880 (3Dpro), Alien $649 (HT) / $849 (ML 2.0) / $1,049 (Extreme), K&A/Backup $650–$750, Mabel copy-mill $999, Glidewell dentist-facing mill-from-file $1,450; Bear's $650 is mid-pack. Value: $580–$1,049 lab-to-lab; $1,450 Glidewell dentist-facing. [source](https://www.alienmilling.com/collections/full-arch-hybrid-implant-zirconia) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* Lab-to-lab titanium bars: Alien $399 flat regardless of implant count (MUA interfaces only), Monster $350 flat (+$280 design from model scan or +$400 from IOS; MUAs $18/unit), Ivorymade $650/arch, Mabel $699 (bar/framework to master model); dentist-facing: ROE $825, Glidewell $923; Bear's $299 std / $399 rush is at the low end. Value: $350–$699 lab-to-lab; $825–$923 dentist-facing. [source](https://www.alienmilling.com/products/alien-implant-titanium-bar) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* Mabel Dental Lab (Ohio) Jan 2026 lab-to-lab price list is a full outsourcing benchmark: full-contour zirconia $59 (coping $39), e.max $69, Ti custom abutment $119, screw-retained full zirconia $189, zirconia full-arch implant prosthesis $1,999 (incl. up to 6 MUAs and 2 try-ins), copy-mill full-arch $999, CAD/CAM Ti bar $699, screw-retained hybrid denture $1,299, zirconia over hybrid bar $2,999, night guard hard/soft $65; 6 in-lab days digital; shipping $9 each way. Value: FCZ $59; full-arch $1,999; Ti bar $699. [source](https://mabeldental.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Mabel-Dental-Lab-to-Lab-1-2026-Fee-Schedule.pdf) (2026-01)
- *fact, high confidence.* National-network labs operate inside Integra's home market: NDX Issaquah (640 NW Gilman Blvd, Issaquah WA 98027, 'digital design and on-site milling'), NDX Advanced Dental Technologies (11820 NE Crestwood Dr, Vancouver WA), Modern Dental Center Seattle (330 112th Ave NE Ste 101, Bellevue WA 98004), MicroDental Northwest (1123 N Grant Pl, Kennewick WA). Value: 4 national-network labs in WA. [source](https://author1.nationaldentex.com/ndxissaquah/home) (undated)
- *fact, medium confidence.* DDS Lab (Tampa) United Concordia network schedule (document references Feb 1, 2025 alloy policy): zirconia solid $69 ($64 digital), zirconia HT $70, lithium disilicate $75 ($64 digital), temp crown $32, shipping $6.99/case, night guard hard $70 / soft $50, rush $150/unit; Dandy's table lists DDS at 12-day turnaround and 1-year warranty. Value: zirconia $64–69; e.max $64–75; shipping $6.99. [source](https://go.ddslab.com/hubfs/02%20Sales%20Collateral/03%20Fee%20Schedules/Standard%20%2B%20UC%20pricing/DDSLab_FeeSchedule_UC2020.pdf) (2020 (updated note dated 2025-02-01))
- *fact, high confidence.* ROE Dental Lab (Ohio) 2026 fee schedule (REV100726) is a well-run digital-lab comp: zRc zirconia $93 model-free (3 days), TLZ zirconia $122, e.max $134; TLZ-SR screw-retained crown $299 model-free (4 days); Ti/Zr abutment + TLZ crown $411 model-free (7 days); TLZ-IB coping-free full-arch zirconia final $2,420 (10 days); titanium bar $825 (11 days); CLEARguard splints $69–$111; tax and shipping not included. Value: zirconia $93; SR crown $299; full-arch $2,420; Ti bar $825. [source](https://www.roedentallab.com/hubfs/47353199/PDFs/Fee-Schedule-ROE.pdf) (2026 (REV100726))
- *fact, medium confidence.* Low-price national shippers targeting the Pacific Northwest: Next Dental Lab (Deerfield Beach FL) advertises $39 zirconia, $47 e.max, All-on-X with Ti bar from $1,795 to Oregon practices with ~4 business days ground each way and prepaid labels, and claims typical Oregon lab fees of $100–$185 for a zirconia crown; TrueGuide Lab (NJ) lists $99 zirconia, $139 e.max, $349 SR crown on Ti-base, $399 custom abutment + crown, All-on-X from $2,999, 5–7 business days, free ground on 3+ units. Value: $39 zirconia; $1,795 All-on-X w/ Ti bar. [source](https://signup.nextdentallab.com/dental-lab/oregon) (undated)
- *unknown, medium confidence.* Milling-center pricing that is NOT public: Argen Digital (San Diego; 140,000 sq ft, 150 machines, 2-business-day Ti abutments/bars), Core3dcentres USA (Las Vegas), Jensen Dental, Zahn/Henry Schein, Preat (patient-specific bars), Panthera Dental (Quebec; Fusion Bar with split overlay STL), Zirkonzahn (components with up to 30-year warranty), Evolve Dentistry (pricing page removed), DLMS and ProtoDent (no price pages found); Strategy Milling (Leetsdale PA) publishes only its gold milling fee of $29.95/unit plus alloy and claims an external remake rate below 0.5%. Value: n/a (quote/login only). [source](https://argen.com/store/brochures/59c99e29-2483-43ed-9fbc-363170823248) (undated)
- *historical, medium confidence.* Oral Arts (Huntsville AL) historical 2017 Smile Source schedule: Element-Z solid zirconia $99, e.max $99, crown with custom Ti/Zr abutment $368, model-less screw-retained crown $248, 5-day standard turnaround, free shipping both ways; current pricing not published. Value: $99 zirconia; free shipping both ways. [source](https://www.oralartsdentalcr.com/wp-content/uploads/smile_source_feeSchedule_6_13_17.pdf) (2017-06-13)

**Competitors mapped.**

### B.12 Tri Niche

*Scope:* TRI Dental Implants matrix abutment-free system as a niche demand funnel for a certified lab (Integra Dental Design, Bellevue WA)

**Agent summary.** TRI matrix is real, FDA-cleared, and still small in the US; certification is worth finishing but is not a national demand engine for Integra in 6-12 months. FACTS. matrix is TRI Dental Implants Int. AG's (Hunenberg, CH; founded 2011 by ex-Straumann/Implant Direct exec Tobias Richter) implant connection on which a CAD/CAM zirconia or titanium "Crown Abutment" screws directly to the implant with no Ti-base, abutment or cement. CE mark Oct 2020; FDA 510(k) K203660 cleared 21 Dec 2021; US-only line extension matrix X-Force (K242661) cleared 8 Jan 2025 and launched Dec 2025. TRI's own lead-user survey: 2,334 matrix implants placed worldwide Oct 2020-Dec 2022 (99.7% success). LinkedIn lists ~43 employees, ~10 in the US; investors are SHS Capital (since Sept 2014) and Geistlich Pharma (minority stake Sept 2025, CEO on TRI's board). The US entity "TRI Dental Implants USA Inc." is published at a Dover, DE registered-agent address; US phone lines use the 949 (Orange County) area code, including a dedicated lab line (949-210-3877), and RocketReach shows a sales employee in Irvine, but no Orange County street address or "TRI Digital Hub" address is published anywhere I could find. Patterson Dental became authorized US distributor in March 2023; TRI reports a direct US sales structure since Oct 2024 and was hiring reps for Boston, NJ/Philadelphia, SF Bay, LA (Jan 2026) and Arizona (Sept 2026). US leadership: Dereck Fandel (EVP Sales & Operations), David Gentile (Director of Sales), Corey Kabot (Eastern US Area Sales Director), Brett Jackson (Area Sales Director), Amir Gordji DDS (Sr Director Clinical Affairs). A competitor (Gerald Niznick, Dec 2023) cited $325 per matrix implant. CERTIFICATION. TRI publishes no requirements, fee or lab count; the certified-lab list is behind a request form. What is public: the IFU requires TRI interface files (free on request), specified screws, zirconia with at least 1,200 MPa three-point bending strength, verification of every suprastructure with TRI verification tools (VTool-P37/P45) and "appropriate measuring equipment"; the matrix Academy has a technician certification track "from CAD installation to CAM strategies" (free registration code). Regulatory nuance: the 510(k) states patient-specific crown abutments are manufactured "at TRI Dental Implants Int. AG facilities" from design files, and a separate Talladium 510(k) (K241170, Oct 2024) for TRI-matrix screws describes manufacturing "at a Talladium-validated milling center." In FDA terms a validated milling center is a contract manufacturer that must register its establishment (FY2026 fee $11,423), list devices and run a quality system. Integra should confirm with TRI whether "certified manufacturing center" carries these obligations. HOW DEMAND IS ROUTED. TRI's Tooth-in-a-Box / Bridge-in-a-Box / Arch-in-a-Box bundles include the final zirconia "designed and manufactured by the TRI lab network"; TRI coordinates, bills one fixed price and the partner lab gives a 10-year warranty. TRI also sells Scan & Smile "Prosthetic Services for Labs" and co-brands full-arch bundles with InstaRisa (Dr. Art Mirelez, Clovis CA). So TRI is itself the aggregator: certified labs are suppliers to TRI's bundle as much as recipients of dentist referrals. Glidewell (Irvine CA) is the publicly announced US certified manufacturing center (Apr 2025) with "special pricing" and a lifetime BruxZir warranty; Rutherford Dental Lab (Livermore CA) advertises TRI matrix workflows nationwide; JB Dental Lab (Tempe AZ) hosts TRI FP1 courses. The 3-on-6 FP1 movement (Smile Systems, Utah; providers in UT, MA, CO, AZ, OK, AK, MI, FL, NM, ID) runs on TRI implants but is restored exclusively by Smile Systems' own lab. Several TRI US KOLs run in-house labs (Mirelez/Clovis, Azam Saeed/Chicago ~100 arches per month, Chris Winterholler/Scottsdale). No Washington, Oregon, Alaska or Hawaii TRI KOL, course, certified lab or practice advertising matrix surfaced. ESTIMATES (labeled). US dentists actively placing matrix: unknown; 150-500 accounts is my estimate from 5-7 field reps at 25-75 active accounts each (low confidence). US matrix implants per year: unknown; low thousands is plausible given 2,334 worldwide in the first 27 months. Integra's plausible matrix lab volume: 0-5 units/month at 6 months and 3-15 units/month at 12 months if certified, listed and actively marketed; upside only if Integra becomes a Tooth-in-a-Box subcontractor (price set by TRI) or lands one of the ~20 identifiable US TRI practices that lacks an in-house lab. At Integra's $129-$299 per unit that is roughly $1,000-$4,000/month, useful but not transformative against ~$11,700/month revenue. DEPENDENCIES AND RISKS. Certification not yet complete (Oct 2026). Possible FDA contract-manufacturer obligations. Glidewell's scale, pricing and warranty. TRI's small US footprint and unproven volume (its German subsidiary posted losses every year 2018-2024; LinkedIn's sub-$1M revenue estimate is unreliable either way). KOL in-house labs and Smile Systems' captive lab remove the highest-volume users from the open market. TRI's own lab services compete for the same work. Single-brand dependency. Clinical debate (Niznick's screw-loosening critique; a 2025 FEA paper found higher stresses in the abutment-free model) could slow adoption. VERDICT. Finish the certification because it is cheap and strategically useful: it positions Integra as the Pacific Northwest's only certified matrix lab if TRI ever sells there, gives a credible reason to court TRI's California KOLs (Mirelez, Dunlop, Wasserstein, Shah), and dovetails with the ARUM/MillBox business, since TRI's "mill locally" pitch creates equipment demand. Do not model it as a demand engine; model it as 3-15 units/month at month 12 with meaningful probability of zero.

**Findings.**

- *fact, high confidence.* matrix is an implant connection for direct-to-implant CAD/CAM zirconia or titanium restorations with no abutment, Ti-base or cement; CE-marked Oct 2020, first shown at IDS 2019. Value: CE mark Oct 2020. [source](https://www.dental-tribune.com/news/worlds-first-digital-implant-receives-european-ce-mark-certification/) (2020-10-20)
- *fact, high confidence.* FDA 510(k) K203660 for the TRI-matrix Implant Line was cleared 21 Dec 2021; the 'Crown Abutment' is a patient-specific CAD/CAM part that also serves as the final restoration. Value: K203660; decision 2021-12-21. [source](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/cdrh_docs/pdf20/K203660.pdf) (2021-12-21)
- *fact, high confidence.* matrix X-Force (immediacy/high primary stability line extension) was FDA-cleared 8 Jan 2025 (K242661) and is sold exclusively in the United States. Value: K242661. [source](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/scripts/cdrh/cfdocs/cfPMN/pmn.cfm?ID=K242661) (2025-01-08)
- *historical, medium confidence.* TRI's lead-user survey: 2,334 matrix implants placed worldwide Oct 2020-Dec 2022 with 99.7% success; a second survey was run Dec 2024 (4.5 years). Value: 2,334 implants in 27 months, 99.7% success. [source](https://tri-implants.swiss/download/2026-02-tri_-matrix-overview-en-01_web.pdf) (2026-02 (catalog code))
- *fact, high confidence.* TRI publishes no certified-lab list, requirements or fees; the 'matrix Certified Lab List' is available only by submitting a contact form. Value: gated. [source](https://info.tri-implants.swiss/matrixlabsoverview) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* Lab-side requirements in the TRI IFU: TRI interface files (free on request), specified screws, zirconia >=1200 MPa, verification of every suprastructure with a TRI verification tool (VTool-P37 / VTool-P45) and 'appropriate measuring equipment'; warnings against worn CAM tools, inappropriate CAM strategies and inappropriate machines. Value: zirconia >=1200 MPa; VTool-P37/P45. [source](https://www.adm1stchoicemedicals.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/04/1-TRI-IMPLANT-SYSTEM_IFU_ENG.pdf) (undated (uploaded 2021-04))
- *fact, high confidence.* TRI's matrix Academy offers a certification program for dental technicians covering 'surgical procedures, CAD installations and CAM strategies', with a free registration code. Value: free registration code. [source](https://tri-implants.swiss/de/academy/matrix-academy/) (undated)
- *fact, medium confidence.* A 'certified manufacturing center' / 'validated milling center' is, in FDA terms, a contract manufacturer for the 510(k) holder and must register its establishment, list devices and operate under quality-system regulations. [source](https://idt.cdeworld.com/courses/5414-in-house-custom-abutment-manufacturing-options) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* FDA annual medical-device establishment registration fee for FY2026 (Oct 1 2025-Sep 30 2026) is $11,423; no small-business reduction applies to registration except hardship waivers. Value: $11,423 per year. [source](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2025-07-30/pdf/2025-14412.pdf) (2025-07-30)
- *fact, high confidence.* TRI itself aggregates final-restoration demand: Tooth-in-a-Box / Bridge-in-a-Box / Arch-in-a-Box bundle the implant, screw and final zirconia 'designed and manufactured by the TRI lab network' at one fixed price, with TRI coordinating and a 10-year partner-lab warranty. Value: fixed price not published. [source](https://tri-implants.swiss/en/workflow/single-crown/) (undated)
- *fact, high confidence.* Glidewell (Irvine/Newport Beach, CA) was designated a certified TRI matrix manufacturing center in April 2025 with 'low, special pricing' and a lifetime BruxZir warranty; it publishes a TRI Matrix Implant Rx. **Competitors mapped.**
